FILE: app\api\admin\waitlist\growth\route.ts
------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server'
import { db } from '@/lib/db'
import { requirePermission } from '@/lib/rbac-guard'
import type { WaitlistGrowthPeriod, WaitlistGrowthPoint } from '@/types/waitlist'

const BUCKET_COUNT: Record<WaitlistGrowthPeriod, number> = {
  daily: 30,
  weekly: 12,
  monthly: 12,
}

export async function GET(request: Request) {
  const guard = await requirePermission('waitlist:view')
  if (!guard.ok) return guard.response

  const { searchParams } = new URL(request.url)
  const period = (searchParams.get('period') ?? 'daily') as WaitlistGrowthPeriod

  if (!['daily', 'weekly', 'monthly'].includes(period)) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid period' }, { status: 400 })
  }

  try {
    const { rows } = await db.query('SELECT * FROM get_waitlist_growth($1, $2)', [
      period,
      BUCKET_COUNT[period],
    ])

    const baseResult = await db.query('SELECT COUNT(*) FROM waitlist WHERE created_at < $1', [
      rows[0]?.bucket ?? new Date(),
    ])
    let cumulative = Number(baseResult.rows[0]?.count ?? 0)

    const chart: WaitlistGrowthPoint[] = rows.map((row: { bucket: string; signups: string }) => {
      cumulative += Number(row.signups)
      return { bucket: row.bucket, signups: Number(row.signups), cumulative }
    })

    return NextResponse.json({ chart, period })
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('[waitlist/growth]', error)
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Failed to load waitlist growth' }, { status: 500 })
  }
}

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: app\api\admin\waitlist\kpis\route.ts
------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server'
import { db } from '@/lib/db'
import { requirePermission } from '@/lib/rbac-guard'
import type { WaitlistKpis } from '@/types/waitlist'

export async function GET(request: Request) {
  const guard = await requirePermission('waitlist:view')
  if (!guard.ok) return guard.response

  try {
    const { rows } = await db.query('SELECT * FROM get_waitlist_kpis()')
    const r = rows[0]

    const sparklineResult = await db.query(
      `SELECT date_trunc('day', created_at) AS day, COUNT(*) AS count
       FROM waitlist
       WHERE created_at >= now() - interval '7 days'
       GROUP BY 1
       ORDER BY 1`
    )
    const byDay = new Map<string, number>(
      sparklineResult.rows.map((row) => [
        new Date(row.day).toISOString().slice(0, 10),
        Number(row.count),
      ])
    )
    const sparkline_7d: number[] = []
    for (let i = 6; i >= 0; i--) {
      const d = new Date()
      d.setDate(d.getDate() - i)
      sparkline_7d.push(byDay.get(d.toISOString().slice(0, 10)) ?? 0)
    }

    const topSourceResult = await db.query(
      `SELECT COALESCE(utm_source, 'direct') AS source, COUNT(*) AS count
       FROM waitlist
       GROUP BY 1
       ORDER BY count DESC
       LIMIT 1`
    )

    const pctChange = (today: number, prev: number) =>
      prev === 0 ? (today > 0 ? 100 : 0) : Math.round(((today - prev) / prev) * 1000) / 10

    const payload: WaitlistKpis = {
      total_signups: Number(r.total_signups),
      signups_today: Number(r.signups_today),
      signups_today_change_pct: pctChange(Number(r.signups_today), Number(r.signups_yesterday)),
      signups_this_week: Number(r.signups_this_week),
      signups_this_week_change_pct: pctChange(
        Number(r.signups_this_week),
        Number(r.signups_last_week)
      ),
      pending_count: Number(r.pending_count),
      invited_count: Number(r.invited_count),
      converted_count: Number(r.converted_count),
      conversion_rate:
        Number(r.total_signups) === 0
          ? 0
          : Math.round((Number(r.converted_count) / Number(r.total_signups)) * 1000) / 10,
      top_utm_source: topSourceResult.rows[0]?.source ?? null,
      sparkline_7d,
    }

    return NextResponse.json(payload)
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('[waitlist/kpis]', error)
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Failed to load waitlist KPIs' }, { status: 500 })
  }
}

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: app\api\admin\waitlist\route.ts
------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server'
import { db } from '@/lib/db'
import { requirePermission } from '@/lib/rbac-guard'
import { parseUserAgent } from '@/lib/parse-user-agent'
import type { WaitlistEntryEnriched, WaitlistListResponse } from '@/types/waitlist'

const ALLOWED_SORT = new Set(['created_at', 'position', 'email'])

export async function GET(request: Request) {
  const guard = await requirePermission('waitlist:view')
  if (!guard.ok) return guard.response

  const { searchParams } = new URL(request.url)
  const page = Math.max(1, Number(searchParams.get('page') ?? 1))
  const pageSize = Math.min(100, Math.max(10, Number(searchParams.get('pageSize') ?? 25)))
  const search = searchParams.get('search')?.trim()
  const status = searchParams.get('status')
  const utmSource = searchParams.get('utm_source')
  const dateFrom = searchParams.get('dateFrom')
  const dateTo = searchParams.get('dateTo')
  const sortByRaw = searchParams.get('sortBy') ?? 'position'
  const sortColumn = ALLOWED_SORT.has(sortByRaw) ? sortByRaw : 'position'
  const sortDir = searchParams.get('sortDir') === 'desc' ? 'DESC' : 'ASC'

  const conditions: string[] = []
  const params: unknown[] = []

  if (search) {
    params.push(`%${search}%`)
    conditions.push(`email ILIKE $${params.length}`)
  }
  if (status && status !== 'all') {
    params.push(status)
    conditions.push(`status = $${params.length}`)
  }
  if (utmSource && utmSource !== 'all') {
    if (utmSource === 'direct') {
      conditions.push('utm_source IS NULL')
    } else {
      params.push(utmSource)
      conditions.push(`utm_source = $${params.length}`)
    }
  }
  if (dateFrom) {
    params.push(dateFrom)
    conditions.push(`created_at >= $${params.length}`)
  }
  if (dateTo) {
    params.push(dateTo)
    conditions.push(`created_at <= $${params.length}`)
  }

  const whereClause = conditions.length ? `WHERE ${conditions.join(' AND ')}` : ''

  try {
    const countResult = await db.query(`SELECT COUNT(*) FROM waitlist ${whereClause}`, params)
    const total = Number(countResult.rows[0].count)

    const offset = (page - 1) * pageSize
    const dataParams = [...params, pageSize, offset]

    const dataResult = await db.query(
      `SELECT * FROM waitlist ${whereClause}
       ORDER BY ${sortColumn} ${sortDir}
       LIMIT $${dataParams.length - 1} OFFSET $${dataParams.length}`,
      dataParams
    )

    const enriched: WaitlistEntryEnriched[] = dataResult.rows.map((row) => {
      const parsed = parseUserAgent(row.user_agent)
      return {
        ...row,
        is_bot: parsed.isBot,
        device_type: parsed.deviceType,
        browser: parsed.browser,
        os: parsed.os,
      }
    })

    const payload: WaitlistListResponse = { rows: enriched, total, page, pageSize }
    return NextResponse.json(payload)
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('[waitlist/list]', error)
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Failed to load waitlist entries' }, { status: 500 })
  }
}

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: app\api\admin\waitlist\utm-breakdown\route.ts
------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server'
import { db } from '@/lib/db'
import { requirePermission } from '@/lib/rbac-guard'
import type { UtmBreakdownRow } from '@/types/waitlist'

export async function GET(request: Request) {
  const guard = await requirePermission('waitlist:view')
  if (!guard.ok) return guard.response

  try {
    const { rows } = await db.query('SELECT * FROM get_utm_breakdown()')
    const total = rows.reduce((sum: number, r: { count: string }) => sum + Number(r.count), 0)

    const payload: UtmBreakdownRow[] = rows.map((r: { utm_source: string | null; count: string }) => ({
      utm_source: r.utm_source,
      count: Number(r.count),
      pct: total === 0 ? 0 : Math.round((Number(r.count) / total) * 1000) / 10,
    }))

    return NextResponse.json({ rows: payload, total })
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('[waitlist/utm-breakdown]', error)
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Failed to load UTM breakdown' }, { status: 500 })
  }
}

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: db\migrations\003_waitlist_functions.sql
------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 003_waitlist_functions.sql

-- Enables fast ILIKE search on email (used by the search box).
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS pg_trgm;

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_waitlist_status      ON waitlist (status);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_waitlist_utm_source  ON waitlist (utm_source);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_waitlist_created_at  ON waitlist (created_at);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_waitlist_position    ON waitlist (position);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_waitlist_email_trgm  ON waitlist USING gin (email gin_trgm_ops);

-- 3.1 KPI aggregate â€” single round trip for the whole KPI row
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION get_waitlist_kpis()
RETURNS TABLE (
  total_signups      bigint,
  signups_today       bigint,
  signups_yesterday   bigint,
  signups_this_week   bigint,
  signups_last_week   bigint,
  pending_count       bigint,
  invited_count       bigint,
  converted_count     bigint
) AS $$
  SELECT
    (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM waitlist),
    (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM waitlist
       WHERE created_at >= date_trunc('day', now())),
    (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM waitlist
       WHERE created_at >= date_trunc('day', now()) - interval '1 day'
         AND created_at <  date_trunc('day', now())),
    (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM waitlist
       WHERE created_at >= date_trunc('week', now())),
    (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM waitlist
       WHERE created_at >= date_trunc('week', now()) - interval '7 days'
         AND created_at <  date_trunc('week', now())),
    (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM waitlist WHERE status = 'pending'),
    (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM waitlist WHERE status = 'invited'),
    (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM waitlist WHERE status = 'converted');
$$ LANGUAGE sql STABLE;

-- 3.2 Growth series â€” bucketed signups + running cumulative total
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION get_waitlist_growth(p_period text, p_buckets int)
RETURNS TABLE (bucket timestamptz, signups bigint) AS $$
DECLARE
  v_interval    interval;
  v_trunc_unit  text;
BEGIN
  v_interval := CASE p_period
    WHEN 'weekly'  THEN interval '1 week'
    WHEN 'monthly' THEN interval '1 month'
    ELSE interval '1 day'
  END;

  v_trunc_unit := CASE p_period
    WHEN 'weekly'  THEN 'week'
    WHEN 'monthly' THEN 'month'
    ELSE 'day'
  END;

  RETURN QUERY
  WITH buckets AS (
    SELECT generate_series(
      date_trunc(v_trunc_unit, now()) - (v_interval * (p_buckets - 1)),
      date_trunc(v_trunc_unit, now()),
      v_interval
    ) AS bucket_start
  )
  SELECT
    b.bucket_start,
    COUNT(w.id)
  FROM buckets b
  LEFT JOIN waitlist w
    ON w.created_at >= b.bucket_start
   AND w.created_at <  b.bucket_start + v_interval
  GROUP BY b.bucket_start
  ORDER BY b.bucket_start;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE;

-- 3.3 UTM source breakdown (NULL source bucketed as "direct" traffic)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION get_utm_breakdown()
RETURNS TABLE (utm_source text, count bigint) AS $$
  SELECT utm_source, COUNT(*)
  FROM waitlist
  GROUP BY utm_source
  ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC;
$$ LANGUAGE sql STABLE;

// components/disputes/DisputesSection.tsx
'use client'

import { useState } from 'react'
import { useSearchParams } from 'next/navigation'
import { cn } from '@/lib/cn'
import { DISPUTE_TABS, type DisputeTab, type DisputeView } from '@/lib/dispute-constants'
import { useDisputeCounts, useDisputeFilters, useDisputeList } from '@/hooks/useDisputes'
import { useWorkerFilterOptions } from '@/hooks/useWorkers'
import DisputeFilterBar from './DisputeFilterBar'
import DisputeRefundsTab from './DisputeRefundsTab'
import DisputeTable from './DisputeTable'
import { KNOWN_CITIES, Pager } from './dispute-ui'

const TAB_LABEL: Record<DisputeTab, string> = {
  open: 'Open',
  awaiting: 'Awaiting response',
  escalated: 'Escalated',
  resolved: 'Resolved (30d)',
}

const TAB_HINT: Record<DisputeTab, string> = {
  open: 'Needs an admin decision. Oldest first. Rows turn amber after 48h and red after 72h.',
  awaiting: 'Waiting for the worker to answer. They get 24h before it moves to Open.',
  escalated: 'Flagged for Super Admin review. Only a Super Admin can resolve these.',
  resolved: 'Decisions from the last 30 days, newest first.',
}

const VIEW_LABEL: Record<DisputeView, string> = {
  disputes: 'Disputes',
  refunds: 'Refunds',
}

function DisputeQueue({ initialTab }: { initialTab?: DisputeTab }) {
  const { filters, update, reset } = useDisputeFilters(initialTab ? { tab: initialTab } : {})
  const { disputes, isLoading, error, mutate } = useDisputeList(filters)
  const { counts } = useDisputeCounts()
  const { categories } = useWorkerFilterOptions()

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
      <div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1 border-b border-gray-200">
          {DISPUTE_TABS.map((tab) => {
            const active = filters.tab === tab
            return (
              <button
                key={tab}
                onClick={() => update({ tab })}
                className={cn(
                  'flex items-center gap-2 border-b-2 px-3 py-2 text-sm transition-colors duration-150',
                  active
                    ? 'border-[#1F6F5F] font-medium text-[#1F6F5F]'
                    : 'border-transparent text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-700'
                )}
              >
                {TAB_LABEL[tab]}
                <span className="rounded-full bg-gray-100 px-1.5 text-xs text-gray-600">
                  {counts ? counts[tab].toLocaleString('en-PK') : 'â€“'}
                </span>
              </button>
            )
          })}
        </div>
        <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-gray-500">{TAB_HINT[filters.tab]}</p>
      </div>

      <DisputeFilterBar
        filters={filters}
        onChange={update}
        onReset={reset}
        cities={KNOWN_CITIES}
        categories={categories}
      />

      <DisputeTable
        tab={filters.tab}
        rows={disputes?.data ?? []}
        isLoading={isLoading}
        error={error}
        onRetry={() => mutate()}
      />

      {disputes && (
        <Pager
          page={filters.page}
          pageSize={filters.pageSize}
          total={disputes.total}
          onPage={(page) => update({ page })}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

export default function DisputesSection() {
  const searchParams = useSearchParams()
  const tabParam = searchParams.get('tab')
  const validTab = (DISPUTE_TABS as readonly string[]).includes(tabParam ?? '')

  const [view, setView] = useState<DisputeView>(
    searchParams.get('view') === 'refunds' ? 'refunds' : 'disputes'
  )

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
      <div className="flex w-fit rounded-md border border-gray-300 bg-white p-0.5">
        {(['disputes', 'refunds'] as const).map((v) => (
          <button
            key={v}
            onClick={() => setView(v)}
            className={cn(
              'h-8 rounded px-4 text-sm transition-colors duration-150',
              view === v
                ? 'bg-[#1F6F5F]/10 font-medium text-[#1F6F5F]'
                : 'text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-700'
            )}
          >
            {VIEW_LABEL[v]}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      {view === 'disputes' ? (
        <DisputeQueue initialTab={validTab ? (tabParam as DisputeTab) : undefined} />
      ) : (
        <DisputeRefundsTab />
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

// components/disputes/DisputeTable.tsx
'use client'

import Link from 'next/link'
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'
import DataTable, { DataTableColumn } from '@/components/shared/DataTable'
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/Badge'
import type { DisputeTab } from '@/lib/dispute-constants'
import type { DisputeListItem } from '@/types/dispute'
import {
  Person,
  RESOLUTION_LABEL,
  RESOLUTION_TONE,
  SLA_ROW_CLASS,
  SLA_TONE,
  STATUS_LABEL,
  STATUS_TONE,
  formatAge,
  formatDateTime,
  formatPkr,
  shortId,
  slaLevel,
} from './dispute-ui'

interface Props {
  tab: DisputeTab
  rows: DisputeListItem[]
  isLoading: boolean
  error: unknown
  onRetry: () => void
}

const EMPTY: Record<DisputeTab, string> = {
  open: 'No disputes waiting for a decision. Nice work.',
  awaiting: 'No disputes waiting on a worker response.',
  escalated: 'Nothing escalated to a Super Admin.',
  resolved: 'No disputes resolved in the last 30 days.',
}

export default function DisputeTable({ tab, rows, isLoading, error, onRetry }: Props) {
  const router = useRouter()
  const isResolvedTab = tab === 'resolved'

  const baseColumns: DataTableColumn<DisputeListItem>[] = [
    {
      key: 'dispute',
      header: 'Dispute',
      render: (d) => (
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
          <span className="font-mono text-xs text-gray-700">{shortId(d.id)}</span>
          {d.openedByAdmin && <Badge tone="info">Admin-opened</Badge>}
          {d.isEscalated && !isResolvedTab && <Badge tone="danger">Escalated</Badge>}
        </div>
      ),
    },
    {
      key: 'booking',
      header: 'Booking',
      render: (d) => (
        <Link
          href={`/bookings/${d.bookingId}`}
          onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
          className="font-mono text-xs text-gray-700 hover:text-[#1F6F5F] hover:underline"
        >
          {shortId(d.bookingId)}
        </Link>
      ),
    },
    { key: 'customer', header: 'Raised by', render: (d) => <Person name={d.userName} phone={d.userPhone} /> },
    { key: 'worker', header: 'Against', render: (d) => <Person name={d.workerName} phone={d.workerPhone} /> },
    {
      key: 'category',
      header: 'Category',
      render: (d) => (
        <div className="leading-tight">
          <span>{d.categoryName ?? d.categoryId}</span>
          {d.city && <span className="block text-[11px] text-gray-400">{d.city}</span>}
        </div>
      ),
    },
    { key: 'amount', header: 'At stake', render: (d) => formatPkr(d.amount, d.currency) },
    { key: 'opened', header: 'Opened', render: (d) => formatDateTime(d.createdAt) },
  ]

  const activeColumns: DataTableColumn<DisputeListItem>[] = [
    {
      key: 'age',
      header: 'Age',
      render: (d) => <Badge tone={SLA_TONE[slaLevel(d.createdAt)]}>{formatAge(d.createdAt)}</Badge>,
    },
    {
      key: 'assigned',
      header: 'Assigned',
      render: (d) =>
        d.assignedToName ? (
          <span className="text-gray-800">{d.assignedToName}</span>
        ) : (
          <span className="text-gray-400">Unassigned</span>
        ),
    },
    {
      key: 'status',
      header: 'Status',
      render: (d) => (
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1">
          <Badge tone={STATUS_TONE[d.status]}>{STATUS_LABEL[d.status]}</Badge>
          {d.evidenceCount > 0 && (
            <span className="text-[11px] text-gray-400">{d.evidenceCount} file(s)</span>
          )}
        </div>
      ),
    },
  ]

  const resolvedColumns: DataTableColumn<DisputeListItem>[] = [
    { key: 'resolved', header: 'Resolved', render: (d) => formatDateTime(d.resolvedAt) },
    {
      key: 'outcome',
      header: 'Outcome',
      render: (d) =>
        d.resolution ? (
          <Badge tone={RESOLUTION_TONE[d.resolution]}>{RESOLUTION_LABEL[d.resolution]}</Badge>
        ) : (
          <Badge tone={STATUS_TONE[d.status]}>{STATUS_LABEL[d.status]}</Badge>
        ),
    },
    {
      key: 'refund',
      header: 'Refunded',
      render: (d) => (d.refundAmount && d.refundAmount > 0 ? formatPkr(d.refundAmount, d.currency) : 'â€”'),
    },
    {
      key: 'assigned',
      header: 'Handled by',
      render: (d) => d.assignedToName ?? 'â€”',
    },
  ]

  const actionColumn: DataTableColumn<DisputeListItem> = {
    key: 'action',
    header: '',
    className: 'text-right',
    render: (d) => (
      <Link
        href={`/disputes/${d.id}`}
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
        className={
          isResolvedTab
            ? 'inline-flex h-8 items-center rounded-md border border-gray-300 bg-white px-2.5 text-xs font-medium text-gray-700 transition-colors duration-150 hover:bg-gray-50'
            : 'inline-flex h-8 items-center rounded-md bg-[#1F6F5F] px-2.5 text-xs font-medium text-white transition-colors duration-150 hover:bg-[#195c4f]'
        }
      >
        {isResolvedTab ? 'View' : 'Review'}
      </Link>
    ),
  }

  const columns = [...baseColumns, ...(isResolvedTab ? resolvedColumns : activeColumns), actionColumn]

  return (
    <DataTable
      columns={columns}
      rows={rows}
      rowKey={(d) => d.id}
      isLoading={isLoading}
      error={error}
      onRetry={onRetry}
      emptyMessage={EMPTY[tab]}
      onRowClick={(d) => router.push(`/disputes/${d.id}`)}
      rowClassName={(d) => (isResolvedTab ? '' : SLA_ROW_CLASS[slaLevel(d.createdAt)])}
    />
  )
}

'use client'

import { Input } from '@/components/ui/Input'

            </option>
          ))}

          className={SELECT_CLASS}
          value={filters.categoryId}
          onChange={(e) => onChange({ categoryId: e.target.value })}

          {categories.map((c) => (
            <option key={c.id} value={c.id}>
              {c.name}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
      </Field>

      <Field label="Assigned">
        <select
          className={SELECT_CLASS}
          value={filters.assigned}
          onChange={(e) => onChange({ assigned: e.target.value as DisputeFilters['assigned'] })}
        >
          <option value="all">Anyone</option>
          <option value="me">Assigned to me</option>
          <option value="unassigned">Unassigned</option>
        </select>
      </Field>

      {filters.tab === 'resolved' && (
        <Field label="Outcome">
          <select
            className={SELECT_CLASS}
            value={filters.resolution}
            onChange={(e) => onChange({ resolution: e.target.value as DisputeFilters['resolution'] })}
          >
            <option value="all">Any</option>
            {DISPUTE_RESOLUTIONS.map((r) => (
              <option key={r} value={r}>
                {RESOLUTION_LABEL[r]}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </Field>
      )}

      <Field label="Min amount (PKR)">
        <Input
          type="number"
          min={0}
          className="w-28"
          value={filters.amountMin}
          onChange={(e) => onChange({ amountMin: e.target.value })}
        />
      </Field>
      <Field label="Max amount (PKR)">
        <Input
          type="number"
          min={0}
          className="w-28"

          onChange={(e) => onChange({ amountMax: e.target.value })}
        />
      </Field>

      <Field label="Opened from">
        <Input
          type="date"
          value={filters.dateFrom ?? ''}
          onChange={(e) => onChange({ dateFrom: e.target.value || null })}
        />
      </Field>
      <Field label="Opened to">
        <Input
          type="date"
          value={filters.dateTo ?? ''}
          onChange={(e) => onChange({ dateTo: e.target.value || null })}
        />
      </Field>

      <button onClick={onReset} className="h-9 px-3 text-sm text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-700">
        Clear filters
      </button>
    </div>
  )
}

// components/disputes/DisputeKpiGrid.tsx
'use client'

import { KpiCard, KpiCardSkeleton } from '@/components/dashboard/KpiCard'
import { Card } from '@/components/ui/Card'
import { useDisputeCounts } from '@/hooks/useDisputes'
import { formatPkr } from './dispute-ui'

export function DisputeKpiGrid() {
  const { counts, error, isLoading, mutate } = useDisputeCounts()

  if (error) {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-lg border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-4 text-sm text-red-600">
        Couldn't load dispute stats.{' '}
        <button onClick={() => mutate()} className="underline">
          Retry
        </button>
      </div>
    )
  }

  if (isLoading || !counts) {
    return (
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
        {Array.from({ length: 4 }).map((_, i) => (
          <KpiCardSkeleton key={i} />
        ))}
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
        <KpiCard label="Needs a decision" value={counts.open.toLocaleString('en-PK')} color="#DC2626" />
        <KpiCard label="Awaiting response" value={counts.awaiting.toLocaleString('en-PK')} color="#D97706" />
        <KpiCard label="Escalated" value={counts.escalated.toLocaleString('en-PK')} color="#7C3AED" />
        <KpiCard label="Resolved (30 days)" value={counts.resolved.toLocaleString('en-PK')} color="#16A34A" />
      </div>
      <Card className="flex flex-wrap gap-x-8 gap-y-2 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-gray-600">
        <span>
          Open over 48h: <strong className="text-gray-900">{counts.overdue48h}</strong>
        </span>
        <span>
          Open over 72h: <strong className="text-gray-900">{counts.overdue72h}</strong>
        </span>
        <span>
          Refunded through disputes (30 days):{' '}
          <strong className="text-gray-900">{formatPkr(counts.refundedAmount30d)}</strong>
        </span>
      </Card>
    </div>
  )
}

// components/disputes/dispute-ui.tsx
// Labels, colours, SLA helpers and the 24h response-window logic shared by every dispute component.
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import type { BadgeTone } from '@/components/ui/Badge'
import {
  DISPUTE_SLA_AMBER_HOURS,
  DISPUTE_SLA_RED_HOURS,
  isActiveDispute,
  type DisputeResolution,
  type DisputeStatus,
} from '@/lib/dispute-constants'

export {
  formatDate,
  formatDateTime,
  formatPkr,
  initial,
  shortId,
  timeAgo,
} from '@/components/bookings/booking-ui'
export { Field, KNOWN_CITIES, Pager, Person, SELECT_CLASS } from '@/components/payments/payment-ui'

// ---------- Status / resolution ----------

export const STATUS_LABEL: Record<DisputeStatus, string> = {
  OPEN: 'Open',
  WORKER_RESPONDED: 'Worker responded',
  UNDER_REVIEW: 'Under review',
  RESOLVED: 'Resolved',
  CLOSED: 'Closed',
}

export const STATUS_TONE: Record<DisputeStatus, BadgeTone> = {
  OPEN: 'warning',
  WORKER_RESPONDED: 'info',
  UNDER_REVIEW: 'info',
  RESOLVED: 'success',
  CLOSED: 'neutral',
}

export const RESOLUTION_LABEL: Record<DisputeResolution, string> = {
  REFUND_GRANTED: 'Full refund to customer',
  PARTIAL_REFUND: 'Split decision',
  NO_REFUND: 'Sided with worker',
}

export const RESOLUTION_TONE: Record<DisputeResolution, BadgeTone> = {
  REFUND_GRANTED: 'info',
  PARTIAL_REFUND: 'warning',
  NO_REFUND: 'neutral',
}

// ---------- Age + SLA ----------

export type SlaLevel = 'ok' | 'amber' | 'red'

export function ageHours(iso: string | null | undefined, now: number = Date.now()): number {
  if (!iso) return 0
  return Math.max(0, (now - new Date(iso).getTime()) / 3_600_000)
}

export function slaLevel(iso: string | null | undefined, now: number = Date.now()): SlaLevel {
  const h = ageHours(iso, now)
  if (h >= DISPUTE_SLA_RED_HOURS) return 'red'
  if (h >= DISPUTE_SLA_AMBER_HOURS) return 'amber'
  return 'ok'
}

export function formatAge(iso: string | null | undefined, now: number = Date.now()): string {
  const h = ageHours(iso, now)
  if (h < 1) return '<1h'
  if (h < 24) return `${Math.floor(h)}h`
  const d = Math.floor(h / 24)
  const rem = Math.floor(h % 24)
  return rem ? `${d}d ${rem}h` : `${d}d`
}

export const SLA_TONE: Record<SlaLevel, BadgeTone> = {
  ok: 'neutral',
  amber: 'warning',
  red: 'danger',
}

// DataTable rows have `even:bg-gray-50 hover:bg-gray-50`, so the highlight
// needs important (`!` suffix, Tailwind v4) to win on every row.
export const SLA_ROW_CLASS: Record<SlaLevel, string> = {
  ok: '',
  amber: 'bg-amber-50! even:bg-amber-50! hover:bg-amber-100!',
  red: 'bg-red-50! even:bg-red-50! hover:bg-red-100!',
}

// ---------- Worker response window (24h) ----------

export function formatCountdown(ms: number): string {
  const totalMin = Math.max(0, Math.floor(ms / 60_000))
  const h = Math.floor(totalMin / 60)
  const m = totalMin % 60
  return h > 0 ? `${h}h ${m}m` : `${m}m`
}

export type ResponseKind = 'responded' | 'waiting' | 'expired' | 'closed'

export interface ResponseState {
  kind: ResponseKind
  label: string
  tone: BadgeTone
}

export function responseState(
  d: { status: string; workerResponse: string | null; responseDueAt: string },
  now: number = Date.now()
): ResponseState {
  if (!isActiveDispute(d.status)) return { kind: 'closed', label: 'Closed', tone: 'neutral' }
  if (d.workerResponse) return { kind: 'responded', label: 'Worker responded', tone: 'success' }
  const ms = new Date(d.responseDueAt).getTime() - now
  if (ms > 0) {
    return { kind: 'waiting', label: `${formatCountdown(ms)} left for worker`, tone: 'warning' }
  }
  return { kind: 'expired', label: 'No response Â· window expired', tone: 'danger' }
}

/** Re-renders the caller every `ms` so countdowns stay live. */
export function useNow(ms = 30_000): number {
  const [now, setNow] = useState(() => Date.now())
  useEffect(() => {
    const t = setInterval(() => setNow(Date.now()), ms)
    return () => clearInterval(t)
  }, [ms])
  return now
}

// ---------- Audit trail labels ----------

export const ACTIVITY_LABEL: Record<string, string> = {
  'dispute.claimed': 'took this dispute',
  'dispute.released': 'released this dispute',
  'dispute.escalated': 'escalated to a Super Admin',
  'dispute.resolved': 'resolved the dispute',
  'dispute.note_added': 'added an internal note',
}

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: components\disputes\DisputeResolutionDialogs.tsx

// components/disputes/DisputeResolutionDialogs.tsx
// Resolve (customer / split / worker) and Escalate dialogs.
'use client'

import { useState, type ReactNode } from 'react'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/Button'
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/Input'
import {
  NOTE_MAX_LENGTH,
  roundMoney,
  type DisputeResolution,
} from '@/lib/dispute-constants'
import { disputeActions } from '@/hooks/useDisputes'
import type { DisputeDetail } from '@/types/dispute'
import { formatDateTime, formatPkr, responseState, useNow } from './dispute-ui'

const TEXTAREA_CLASS =
  'h-24 w-full rounded-md border border-gray-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#1F6F5F]'

function Modal({
  title,
  description,
  children,
  error,
  submitting,
  submitLabel,
  submitDisabled,
  destructive,
  onSubmit,
  onClose,
}: {
  title: string
  description?: string
  children: ReactNode
  error: string | null
  submitting: boolean
  submitLabel: string
  submitDisabled: boolean
  destructive: boolean
  onSubmit: () => void
  onClose: () => void
}) {
  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/40 px-4">
      <div className="max-h-[90vh] w-full max-w-md overflow-y-auto rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm">
        <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-gray-900">{title}</h2>
        {description && <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-600">{description}</p>}
        <div className="mt-4 flex flex-col gap-4">{children}</div>
        {error && <p className="mt-3 text-xs text-red-600">{error}</p>}
        <div className="mt-5 flex justify-end gap-2">
          <Button variant="secondary" onClick={onClose} disabled={submitting}>
            Cancel
          </Button>
          <Button
            variant={destructive ? 'destructive' : 'primary'}
            onClick={onSubmit}
            disabled={submitting || submitDisabled}
          >
            {submitting ? 'Workingâ€¦' : submitLabel}
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

function NoteField({
  value,
  onChange,
  label,
  placeholder,
}: {
  value: string
  onChange: (v: string) => void
  label: string
  placeholder?: string
}) {
  return (
    <div>
      <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-medium text-gray-700">{label}</label>
      <textarea
        className={TEXTAREA_CLASS}
        value={value}
        maxLength={NOTE_MAX_LENGTH}
        onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}
        placeholder={placeholder}
      />
    </div>
  )
}

/** Runs the action, closes, surfaces the error. */
function useSubmit(onDone: () => void, onClose: () => void) {
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)

  async function run(fn: () => Promise<unknown>) {
    setError(null)
    setSubmitting(true)
    try {
      await fn()
      onClose()
      onDone()
    } catch (e: any) {
      setError(e?.message || 'Something went wrong')
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false)
    }
  }
  return { submitting, error, run }
}

// ---------- Resolve ----------

export type ResolveMode = 'customer' | 'split' | 'worker'

const TITLE: Record<ResolveMode, string> = {
  customer: 'Resolve for the customer',
  split: 'Split decision',
  worker: 'Resolve for the worker',
}

const DESCRIPTION: Record<ResolveMode, string> = {
  customer:
    'The customer is refunded. If only part of the amount, the rest is released to the worker. Both parties are notified.',
  split:
    'Both sides get part of the money. Set what percentage goes back to the customer. Both parties are notified.',
  worker:
    'No refund. The held payment is released to the worker and the customer is told the decision.',
}

interface ResolveProps {
  mode: ResolveMode
  dispute: DisputeDetail
  onClose: () => void
  onDone: () => void
}

export function ResolveDialog({ mode, dispute, onClose, onDone }: ResolveProps) {
  const now = useNow(30_000)
  const [customerChoice, setCustomerChoice] = useState<'full' | 'partial'>('full')
  const [percent, setPercent] = useState(mode === 'split' ? '50' : '')
  const [note, setNote] = useState('')
  const [override, setOverride] = useState(false)
  const { submitting, error, run } = useSubmit(onDone, onClose)

  const total = dispute.booking.estimatedTotal
  const cur = dispute.booking.currency
  const waiting = responseState(dispute, now).kind === 'waiting'

  const isPartial = mode === 'split' || (mode === 'customer' && customerChoice === 'partial')
  const pct = Number(percent)
  const pctValid = !isPartial || (percent.trim() !== '' && Number.isFinite(pct) && pct > 0 && pct < 100)

  const refund =
    mode === 'worker' ? 0 : isPartial ? (pctValid ? roundMoney((total * pct) / 100) : 0) : total
  const amountValid = !isPartial || (pctValid && refund > 0 && refund < total)

  const resolution: DisputeResolution =
    mode === 'worker' ? 'NO_REFUND' : isPartial ? 'PARTIAL_REFUND' : 'REFUND_GRANTED'

  const remainder = roundMoney(total - refund)

  return (
    <Modal
      title={TITLE[mode]}
      description={DESCRIPTION[mode]}
      error={error}
      submitting={submitting}
      submitLabel="Confirm decision"
      submitDisabled={!note.trim() || !amountValid || (waiting && !override)}
      destructive={false}
      onSubmit={() =>
        run(() =>
          disputeActions.resolve(dispute.id, {
            resolution,
            refundAmount: resolution === 'PARTIAL_REFUND' ? refund : undefined,
            note: note.trim(),
            overrideWindow: waiting && override ? true : undefined,
          })
        )
      }
      onClose={onClose}
    >
      {mode === 'customer' && (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
          <label className="text-xs font-medium text-gray-700">How much to refund</label>
          {(
            [
              { key: 'full', label: 'Full refund (100%)', hint: 'The customer gets everything back.' },
              { key: 'partial', label: 'Partial refund', hint: 'Choose a percentage below.' },
            ] as const
          ).map((o) => (
            <label
              key={o.key}
              className={`flex cursor-pointer items-start gap-2 rounded-md border p-2.5 text-sm ${
                customerChoice === o.key ? 'border-[#1F6F5F] bg-[#1F6F5F]/5' : 'border-gray-200'
              }`}
            >
              <input
                type="radio"
                name="customer-refund"
                className="mt-0.5 accent-[#1F6F5F]"
                checked={customerChoice === o.key}
                onChange={() => setCustomerChoice(o.key)}
              />
              <span>
                <span className="font-medium text-gray-900">{o.label}</span>
                <span className="block text-xs text-gray-400">{o.hint}</span>
              </span>
            </label>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      {isPartial && (
        <Input
          label={
            mode === 'split'
              ? 'Percentage returned to the customer'
              : 'Percentage refunded to the customer'
          }
          type="number"
          min={1}
          max={99}
          step="1"
          placeholder="e.g. 50"
          value={percent}
          onChange={(e) => setPercent(e.target.value)}
          error={percent.trim() !== '' && !pctValid ? 'Enter a percentage between 1 and 99' : undefined}
          helperText={percent.trim() === '' || pctValid ? 'Use "Resolve for the customer" with full refund for 100%.' : undefined}
        />
      )}

      <div className="rounded-md bg-gray-50 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-gray-700">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <span>Booking total</span>
          <span className="font-medium">{formatPkr(total, cur)}</span>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <span>Refund to customer</span>
          <span className="font-semibold">
            {amountValid ? formatPkr(refund, cur) : 'â€”'}
          </span>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <span>Released to worker side</span>
          <span className="font-medium">{amountValid ? formatPkr(remainder, cur) : 'â€”'}</span>
        </div>
        <p className="mt-1 text-[11px] text-gray-400">
          payment-service works out the final worker payout, including the platform fee.
        </p>
        {!dispute.booking.isPaymentConfirmed && (
          <p className="mt-1.5 text-xs text-amber-700">
            Payment isn't confirmed on this booking (likely cash on delivery), so there may be no
            online money to move.
          </p>
        )}
      </div>

      {waiting && (
        <label className="flex cursor-pointer items-start gap-2 rounded-md bg-amber-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-amber-800">
          <input
            type="checkbox"
            className="mt-0.5 h-4 w-4 accent-[#1F6F5F]"
            checked={override}
            onChange={(e) => setOverride(e.target.checked)}
          />
          <span>
            Resolve without waiting for the worker.
            <span className="block text-amber-700">
              They can still respond until {formatDateTime(dispute.responseDueAt)}. Ticking this is
              written to the audit log.
            </span>
          </span>
        </label>
      )}

      <NoteField
        value={note}
        onChange={setNote}
        label="Resolution note (logged, and included in the notification)"
        placeholder="e.g. Photos and chat show the leak was not fixed. Refunding in full."
      />

      <p className="rounded-md bg-red-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-red-700">
        This is final. Once confirmed it queues the refund / release in payment-service and can't be
        undone from the admin panel.
      </p>
    </Modal>
  )
}

// ---------- Escalate ----------

export function EscalateDisputeDialog({
  dispute,
  onClose,
  onDone,
}: {
  dispute: DisputeDetail
  onClose: () => void
  onDone: () => void
}) {
  const [note, setNote] = useState('')
  const { submitting, error, run } = useSubmit(onDone, onClose)

  return (
    <Modal
      title="Escalate to a Super Admin"
      description="Moves this dispute to the Escalated tab and clears the assignment. Only a Super Admin can resolve it from there."
      error={error}
      submitting={submitting}
      submitLabel="Escalate"
      submitDisabled={!note.trim()}
      destructive={false}
      onSubmit={() => run(() => disputeActions.escalate(dispute.id, { note: note.trim() }))}
      onClose={onClose}
    >
      <NoteField
        value={note}
        onChange={setNote}
        label="Why does this need a Super Admin?"
        placeholder="e.g. Amount is large and both sides have strong evidence. Needs a second opinion."
      />
    </Modal>
  )
}

// components/disputes/DisputeNotes.tsx
'use client'

import { useState } from 'react'
import { toast } from 'sonner'
import { Card } from '@/components/ui/Card'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/Button'
import { usePermission } from '@/hooks/usePermission'
import { disputeActions, useDisputeNotes } from '@/hooks/useDisputes'
import { formatDateTime } from './dispute-ui'

export default function DisputeNotes({ disputeId }: { disputeId: string }) {
  const { notes, isLoading, error, mutate } = useDisputeNotes(disputeId)
  const canNote = usePermission('disputes:note')
  const [text, setText] = useState('')
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false)

  async function add() {
    if (!text.trim()) return
    setSaving(true)
    try {
      await disputeActions.addNote(disputeId, text.trim())
      setText('')
      toast.success('Note added')
      mutate()
    } catch (e: any) {
      toast.error(e.message)
    } finally {
      setSaving(false)
    }
  }

  return (
    <Card className="p-4">
      <h2 className="mb-1 text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-gray-500">
        Internal notes
      </h2>
      <p className="mb-3 text-xs text-gray-400">
        Only admins can see these. Never shown to the customer or worker.
      </p>

      {canNote && (
        <div className="mb-4">
          <textarea
            className="h-20 w-full rounded-md border border-gray-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#1F6F5F]"
            value={text}
            maxLength={2000}
            onChange={(e) => setText(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="e.g. Called the customer, they confirmed the leak is still there. Waiting on photos."
          />
          <div className="mt-2 flex justify-end">
            <Button size="sm" loading={saving} disabled={!text.trim()} onClick={add}>
              Add note
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {isLoading ? (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
          {Array.from({ length: 2 }).map((_, i) => (
            <div key={i} className="h-12 animate-pulse rounded bg-gray-100" />
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : error ? (
        <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">
          Couldn't load notes.{' '}
          <button onClick={() => mutate()} className="text-[#1F6F5F] hover:underline">
            Retry
          </button>
        </p>
      ) : notes.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">No notes yet.</p>
      ) : (
        <ul className="flex flex-col gap-3">
          {notes.map((n) => (
            <li key={n.id} className="rounded-md bg-gray-50 px-3 py-2">
              <p className="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words text-sm text-gray-800">{n.note}</p>
              <p className="mt-1 text-[11px] text-gray-400">
                {n.adminEmail} Â· {formatDateTime(n.createdAt)}
              </p>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
    </Card>
  )
}

// hooks/useDisputes.ts
import useSWR from 'swr'
import { useState } from 'react'
import { fetcher } from '@/lib/swr-fetcher'
import type { DisputeResolution } from '@/lib/dispute-constants'
import type { ListResponse, RefundRow } from '@/types/payment'
import type {
  DisputeCounts,
  DisputeDetail,
  DisputeFilters,
  DisputeListResponse,
  DisputeNote,
  DisputeRefundFilters,
} from '@/types/dispute'

export const DEFAULT_DISPUTE_FILTERS: DisputeFilters = {
  tab: 'open',
  search: '',
  city: 'all',
  categoryId: 'all',
  assigned: 'all',
  resolution: 'all',
  amountMin: '',
  amountMax: '',
  dateFrom: null,
  dateTo: null,
  page: 1,
  pageSize: 20,
}

export const DEFAULT_DISPUTE_REFUND_FILTERS: DisputeRefundFilters = {
  search: '',
  status: 'all',
  reason: 'all',
  initiatedBy: 'all',
  source: 'dispute',
  dateFrom: null,
  dateTo: null,
  page: 1,
  pageSize: 25,
}

type Primitive = string | number | null | undefined

function toQs(filters: object): string {
  const qs = new URLSearchParams()
  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(filters as Record<string, Primitive>)) {
    if (v === null || v === undefined || v === '' || v === 'all') continue
    qs.set(k, String(v))
  }
  return qs.toString()
}

export function useDisputeCounts() {
  const { data, error, isLoading, mutate } = useSWR<DisputeCounts>(
    '/api/admin/disputes/counts',
    fetcher,
    { refreshInterval: 30_000 }
  )
  return { counts: data, isLoading, error, mutate }
}

export function useDisputeList(filters: DisputeFilters) {
  const { data, error, isLoading, mutate } = useSWR<DisputeListResponse>(
    `/api/admin/disputes?${toQs(filters)}`,
    fetcher,
    { keepPreviousData: true, refreshInterval: 60_000 }
  )
  return { disputes: data, isLoading, error, mutate }
}

export function useDisputeFilters(initial: Partial<DisputeFilters> = {}) {
  const [filters, setFilters] = useState<DisputeFilters>({ ...DEFAULT_DISPUTE_FILTERS, ...initial })

  function update(patch: Partial<DisputeFilters>) {
    // any real change (tab, search, city...) resets pagination to page 1
    const resetsPage = Object.keys(patch).some((k) => k !== 'page')
    setFilters((prev) => ({ ...prev, ...patch, page: resetsPage ? 1 : (patch.page ?? prev.page) }))
  }

  function reset() {
    setFilters((prev) => ({ ...DEFAULT_DISPUTE_FILTERS, tab: prev.tab }))
  }

  return { filters, update, reset }
}

export function useDisputeDetail(disputeId: string) {
  const { data, error, isLoading, mutate } = useSWR<DisputeDetail>(
    disputeId ? `/api/admin/disputes/${disputeId}` : null,
    fetcher,
    { refreshInterval: 30_000 }
  )
  return { dispute: data, isLoading, error, mutate }
}

export function useDisputeNotes(disputeId: string) {
  const { data, error, isLoading, mutate } = useSWR<DisputeNote[]>(
    disputeId ? `/api/admin/disputes/${disputeId}/notes` : null,
    fetcher
  )
  return { notes: data ?? [], isLoading, error, mutate }
}

export function useDisputeRefunds(filters: DisputeRefundFilters) {
  const { data, error, isLoading, mutate } = useSWR<ListResponse<RefundRow>>(
    `/api/admin/disputes/refunds?${toQs(filters)}`,
    fetcher,
    { keepPreviousData: true, refreshInterval: 60_000 }
  )
  return { refunds: data, isLoading, error, mutate }
}

// --- Mutations ---

async function send(method: 'POST', url: string, body?: object) {
  const res = await fetch(url, {
    method,
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify(body ?? {}),
  })
  const json = await res.json().catch(() => ({}))
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(json.error || 'Action failed')
  return json
}

export const disputeActions = {
  assign: (disputeId: string, action: 'claim' | 'release') =>
    send('POST', `/api/admin/disputes/${disputeId}/actions/assign`, { action }),

  escalate: (disputeId: string, input: { note: string }) =>
    send('POST', `/api/admin/disputes/${disputeId}/actions/escalate`, input),

  resolve: (
    disputeId: string,
    input: {
      resolution: DisputeResolution
      /** Only read by the server for PARTIAL_REFUND. */
      refundAmount?: number
      note: string
      overrideWindow?: boolean
    }
  ) => send('POST', `/api/admin/disputes/${disputeId}/actions/resolve`, input),

  addNote: (disputeId: string, note: string) =>
    send('POST', `/api/admin/disputes/${disputeId}/notes`, { note }),
}

// types/dispute.ts
import type { DisputeResolution, DisputeStatus, DisputeTab } from '@/lib/dispute-constants'
import type { RefundFilters } from '@/types/payment'

// ---------- List ----------

export interface DisputeListItem {
  id: string
  bookingId: string
  status: DisputeStatus
  resolution: DisputeResolution | null
  reason: string
  openedByAdmin: boolean
  isEscalated: boolean
  hasWorkerResponse: boolean
  evidenceCount: number
  refundAmount: number | null
  /** Amount at stake = the booking's quoted total. */
  amount: number
  currency: string
  categoryId: string
  categoryName: string | null
  city: string | null
  userId: string | null
  userName: string | null
  userPhone: string | null
  workerId: string | null
  workerName: string | null
  workerPhone: string | null
  assignedToId: string | null
  assignedToName: string | null
  createdAt: string
  updatedAt: string
  resolvedAt: string | null
}

export interface DisputeListResponse {
  data: DisputeListItem[]
  page: number
  pageSize: number
  total: number
  totalPages: number
}

export interface DisputeFilters {
  tab: DisputeTab
  search: string
  city: string
  categoryId: string
  assigned: 'all' | 'me' | 'unassigned'
  resolution: 'all' | DisputeResolution
  amountMin: string
  amountMax: string
  dateFrom: string | null
  dateTo: string | null
  page: number
  pageSize: number
}

export interface DisputeCounts {
  open: number
  awaiting: number
  escalated: number
  resolved: number
  overdue48h: number
  overdue72h: number
  refundedAmount30d: number
}

// ---------- Detail ----------

  name: string | null
  phone: string | null
  avatarUrl: string | null
}

export interface DisputeRefundRecord {
  id: string
  amount: number
  percentage: number
  reason: string
  status: string
  method: string
  gatewayReference: string | null
  failureReason: string | null
  initiatedAt: string
  completedAt: string | null
}

export interface DisputeActivityRow {
  id: string
  adminEmail: string | null
  action: string
  notes: string | null
  createdAt: string
}

export interface DisputeDetail {
  id: string
  bookingId: string
  status: DisputeStatus
  resolution: DisputeResolution | null
  reason: string
  openedByAdmin: boolean
  evidenceUrls: string[]
  workerResponse: string | null
  adminNote: string | null
  refundAmount: number | null
  createdAt: string
  updatedAt: string
  resolvedAt: string | null
  resolvedByName: string | null
  /** created_at + 24h. After this the dispute no longer waits for the worker. */
  responseDueAt: string

  isEscalated: boolean
  escalatedAt: string | null
  escalatedByName: string | null
  escalationNote: string | null

  assignedTo: { id: string; name: string | null; email: string | null } | null
  assignedAt: string | null
  assignedToMe: boolean

  booking: {
    id: string
    status: string
    categoryId: string
    categoryName: string | null
    city: string | null
    scheduledAt: string
    startedAt: string | null
    completedAt: string | null
    estimatedTotal: number
    platformFee: number
    workerAmount: number
    currency: string
    isPaymentConfirmed: boolean
    paymentIntentId: string | null
  }
  user: DisputeParty
  worker: DisputeParty & { avgRating: number; totalReviews: number }

  refundsAvailable: boolean
  refunds: DisputeRefundRecord[]
  activity: DisputeActivityRow[]
}

export interface DisputeNote {
  id: string
  adminEmail: string
  note: string
  createdAt: string
}

/** 'dispute' = only refunds on bookings that have a dispute. 'everything' = all refunds. */
export type DisputeRefundFilters = RefundFilters & { source: 'dispute' | 'everything' }

// lib/disputes-api.ts
// Server-only helpers for the /api/admin/disputes/* routes.
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server'
import { CITY_SQL, bookingErrorResponse } from '@/lib/bookings-api'
import {
  ADMIN_OPENED_PREFIX,
  DISPUTE_RESOLUTIONS,
  DISPUTE_TABS,
  RESOLVED_WINDOW_DAYS,
  RESPONSE_WINDOW_HOURS,
  type DisputeTab,
} from '@/lib/dispute-constants'
import type { DisputeListItem } from '@/types/dispute'

// Same transaction + outbox helpers the booking actions use.
export {
  enqueueBookingEvent,
  enqueueNotification,
  lockBooking,
  setActor,
  withTx,
} from '@/lib/bookings-api'

const CATEGORY_RE = /^[a-z0-9-]{1,50}$/i
const DATE_RE = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/

// ---------- Errors ----------

export class DisputeActionError extends Error {
  status: number
  constructor(status: number, message: string) {
    super(message)
    this.status = status
  }
}

export const disputeNotFound = () =>
  NextResponse.json({ error: 'Dispute not found' }, { status: 404 })

export function disputeErrorResponse(err: unknown, tag: string) {
  if (err instanceof DisputeActionError) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: err.message }, { status: err.status })
  }
  const code = (err as { code?: string } | null)?.code
  if (code === '42P01' || code === '42703') {
    console.error(tag, err)
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: 'Dispute tables are out of date. Run db/migrations/010_disputes_admin.sql.' },
      { status: 503 }
    )
  }
  // BookingActionError (from lockBooking), unique / exclusion violations, 500s.
  return bookingErrorResponse(err, tag)
}

// ---------- Tab predicates (single source of truth: list AND counts use these) ----------
// They only touch columns of `d` (+ now()), so they also work in `FROM disputes d` count queries.

export const ACTIVE_SQL = `d.status::text IN ('OPEN','WORKER_RESPONDED','UNDER_REVIEW')`
export const RESPONSE_DUE_SQL = `(d.created_at + interval '${RESPONSE_WINDOW_HOURS} hours')`

const AWAITING_SQL = `(d.status::text = 'OPEN' AND d.worker_response IS NULL AND now() < ${RESPONSE_DUE_SQL})`

export const TAB_SQL: Record<DisputeTab, string> = {
  // needs an admin decision now
  open: `${ACTIVE_SQL} AND NOT d.is_escalated AND NOT ${AWAITING_SQL}`,
  // worker still has time to respond
  awaiting: `${AWAITING_SQL} AND NOT d.is_escalated`,
  // flagged for Super Admin
  escalated: `${ACTIVE_SQL} AND d.is_escalated`,
  resolved: `d.status::text IN ('RESOLVED','CLOSED')
             AND COALESCE(d.resolved_at, d.updated_at) >= now() - interval '${RESOLVED_WINDOW_DAYS} days'`,
}

// Active tabs: oldest first (longest waiting on top). Resolved: newest first.
export const TAB_ORDER: Record<DisputeTab, string> = {
  open: 'd.created_at ASC, d.id',
  awaiting: 'd.created_at ASC, d.id',
  escalated: 'd.created_at ASC, d.id',
  resolved: 'd.resolved_at DESC NULLS LAST, d.id',
}

export function parseTab(value: string | null): DisputeTab {
  return (DISPUTE_TABS as readonly string[]).includes(value ?? '') ? (value as DisputeTab) : 'open'
}

// ---------- List SQL ----------

export const DISPUTE_FROM_SQL = `
  FROM disputes d
  JOIN bookings b             ON b.id  = d.booking_id
  LEFT JOIN users u           ON u.id  = d.raised_by
  LEFT JOIN workers w         ON w.id  = d.raised_against
  LEFT JOIN categories c      ON c.id  = b.category_id
  LEFT JOIN user_addresses ua ON ua.id = b.address_id
  LEFT JOIN admin_users aa    ON aa.id = d.assigned_to
`

export const DISPUTE_COLUMNS_SQL = `
  d.id, d.booking_id, d.status::text AS status, d.resolution::text AS resolution,
  d.reason, d.refund_amount, d.is_escalated, d.created_at, d.updated_at, d.resolved_at,
  d.assigned_to, aa.full_name AS assigned_name,
  (d.worker_response IS NOT NULL) AS has_worker_response,
  COALESCE(array_length(d.evidence_urls, 1), 0) AS evidence_count,
  (d.reason LIKE '${ADMIN_OPENED_PREFIX}%') AS opened_by_admin,
  b.estimated_total, b.currency, b.category_id, c.name AS category_name,
  ${CITY_SQL} AS city,
  u.id AS user_id, u.name AS user_name, u.phone AS user_phone,
  w.id AS worker_id, w.name AS worker_name, w.phone AS worker_phone
`

// ---------- Filters ----------

export function buildDisputeWhere(sp: URLSearchParams, adminId: string) {
  const tab = parseTab(sp.get('tab'))
  const where: string[] = [`(${TAB_SQL[tab]})`]
  const params: unknown[] = []
  const push = (value: unknown) => {
    params.push(value)
    return `$${params.length}`
  }

  const search = (sp.get('search') || '').trim()
  const city = sp.get('city') || 'all'
  const categoryId = sp.get('categoryId') || 'all'
  const assigned = sp.get('assigned') || 'all'
  const resolution = (sp.get('resolution') || 'all').toUpperCase()
  const dateFrom = sp.get('dateFrom')
  const dateTo = sp.get('dateTo')
  const min = Number(sp.get('amountMin'))
  const max = Number(sp.get('amountMax'))

  if (search) {
    const p = push(`%${search}%`)
    where.push(
      `(d.id::text ILIKE ${p} OR d.booking_id::text ILIKE ${p} OR u.name ILIKE ${p}
        OR u.phone ILIKE ${p} OR w.name ILIKE ${p} OR w.phone ILIKE ${p})`
    )
  }
  if (city !== 'all') where.push(`${CITY_SQL} = ${push(city)}`)
  if (categoryId !== 'all' && CATEGORY_RE.test(categoryId)) {
    where.push(`b.category_id = ${push(categoryId)}`)
  }
  if (assigned === 'me') where.push(`d.assigned_to = ${push(adminId)}`)
  if (assigned === 'unassigned') where.push('d.assigned_to IS NULL')

  if (tab === 'resolved' && (DISPUTE_RESOLUTIONS as readonly string[]).includes(resolution)) {
    where.push(`d.resolution::text = ${push(resolution)}`)
  }
  if (sp.get('amountMin') && Number.isFinite(min)) where.push(`b.estimated_total >= ${push(min)}`)
  if (sp.get('amountMax') && Number.isFinite(max)) where.push(`b.estimated_total <= ${push(max)}`)

  if (dateFrom && DATE_RE.test(dateFrom)) where.push(`d.created_at >= ${push(dateFrom)}::date`)
  if (dateTo && DATE_RE.test(dateTo)) where.push(`d.created_at < (${push(dateTo)}::date + 1)`)

  return { tab, whereSql: `WHERE ${where.join(' AND ')}`, params }
}

// ---------- Row mapping ----------

const num = (v: unknown) => (v === null || v === undefined ? null : Number(v))

// eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any
export function mapDisputeRow(r: any): DisputeListItem {
  return {
    id: r.id,
    bookingId: r.booking_id,
    status: r.status,
    resolution: r.resolution,
    reason: r.reason,
    openedByAdmin: r.opened_by_admin,
    isEscalated: r.is_escalated,
    hasWorkerResponse: r.has_worker_response,
    evidenceCount: r.evidence_count,
    refundAmount: num(r.refund_amount),
    amount: Number(r.estimated_total ?? 0),
    currency: r.currency ?? 'PKR',
    categoryId: r.category_id,
    categoryName: r.category_name,
    city: r.city,
    userId: r.user_id,
    userName: r.user_name,
    userPhone: r.user_phone,
    workerId: r.worker_id,
    workerName: r.worker_name,
    workerPhone: r.worker_phone,
    assignedToId: r.assigned_to,
    assignedToName: r.assigned_name,
    createdAt: r.created_at,
    updatedAt: r.updated_at,
    resolvedAt: r.resolved_at,
  }
}

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: lib\dispute-constants.ts
------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// lib/dispute-constants.ts
// Shared by server routes AND client components. No server-only imports here.

// ---------- booking-service enums (disputes.status / disputes.resolution) ----------

export const DISPUTE_STATUSES = [
  'OPEN',
  'WORKER_RESPONDED',
  'UNDER_REVIEW',
  'RESOLVED',
  'CLOSED',
] as const
export type DisputeStatus = (typeof DISPUTE_STATUSES)[number]

/** Statuses where an admin decision is still pending. */
export const ACTIVE_DISPUTE_STATUSES: readonly DisputeStatus[] = [
  'OPEN',
  'WORKER_RESPONDED',
  'UNDER_REVIEW',
]

export function isActiveDispute(status: string): boolean {
  return (ACTIVE_DISPUTE_STATUSES as readonly string[]).includes(status)
}

export const DISPUTE_RESOLUTIONS = ['REFUND_GRANTED', 'NO_REFUND', 'PARTIAL_REFUND'] as const
export type DisputeResolution = (typeof DISPUTE_RESOLUTIONS)[number]

// ---------- queue ----------

export const DISPUTE_TABS = ['open', 'awaiting', 'escalated', 'resolved'] as const
export type DisputeTab = (typeof DISPUTE_TABS)[number]

export const DISPUTE_VIEWS = ['disputes', 'refunds'] as const
export type DisputeView = (typeof DISPUTE_VIEWS)[number]

// ---------- rules ----------

/** The worker gets this long to answer before the dispute becomes "Open" for a decision. */
export const RESPONSE_WINDOW_HOURS = 24

/** SLA badges (spec 7.1): amber after 48h, red after 72h. */
export const DISPUTE_SLA_AMBER_HOURS = 48
export const DISPUTE_SLA_RED_HOURS = 72

/** Resolved tab only shows decisions from this many days back. */
export const RESOLVED_WINDOW_DAYS = 30

/** booking escalate action (bookings/[id]/actions/escalate) prefixes the reason with this. */
export const ADMIN_OPENED_PREFIX = '[Opened by admin]'

export const NOTE_MAX_LENGTH = 1000

// ---------- helpers ----------

export const roundMoney = (n: number) => Math.round(n * 100) / 100

export function refundPercentage(total: number, refund: number): number {
  return total > 0 ? roundMoney((refund / total) * 100) : 0
}

/** payment-service test 25: any refund -> booking REFUNDED. Siding with the worker -> RESOLVED. */
export function bookingStatusForResolution(r: DisputeResolution): 'REFUNDED' | 'RESOLVED' {
  return r === 'NO_REFUND' ? 'RESOLVED' : 'REFUNDED'
}

export function stripAdminPrefix(reason: string): string {
  return reason.startsWith(ADMIN_OPENED_PREFIX)
    ? reason.slice(ADMIN_OPENED_PREFIX.length).trim()
    : reason
}

FILE: app\api\admin\disputes\route.ts
------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// app/api/admin/disputes/route.ts
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'
import { db } from '@/lib/db'
import { requirePermission } from '@/lib/rbac-guard'
import {
  DISPUTE_COLUMNS_SQL,
  DISPUTE_FROM_SQL,
  TAB_ORDER,
  buildDisputeWhere,
  disputeErrorResponse,
  mapDisputeRow,
} from '@/lib/disputes-api'

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  const guard = await requirePermission('disputes:view')
  if (!guard.ok) return guard.response

  const sp = req.nextUrl.searchParams
  const { tab, whereSql, params } = buildDisputeWhere(sp, guard.admin.adminId)

  const page = Math.max(1, parseInt(sp.get('page') || '1', 10) || 1)
  const pageSize = Math.min(100, Math.max(1, parseInt(sp.get('pageSize') || '20', 10) || 20))
  const offset = (page - 1) * pageSize

  try {
    const [listRes, countRes] = await Promise.all([
      db.query(
        `SELECT ${DISPUTE_COLUMNS_SQL}
         ${DISPUTE_FROM_SQL}
         ${whereSql}
         ORDER BY ${TAB_ORDER[tab]}
         LIMIT $${params.length + 1} OFFSET $${params.length + 2}`,
        [...params, pageSize, offset]
      ),
      db.query(`SELECT count(*)::int AS total ${DISPUTE_FROM_SQL} ${whereSql}`, params),
    ])

    const total = countRes.rows[0]?.total ?? 0
    return NextResponse.json({
      data: listRes.rows.map(mapDisputeRow),
      page,
      pageSize,
      total,
      totalPages: Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / pageSize)),
    })
  } catch (err) {
    return disputeErrorResponse(err, '[GET /api/admin/disputes]')
  }
}

FILE: app\api\admin\disputes\counts\route.ts
------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// app/api/admin/disputes/counts/route.ts
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server'
import { db } from '@/lib/db'
import { requirePermission } from '@/lib/rbac-guard'
import { DISPUTE_SLA_AMBER_HOURS, DISPUTE_SLA_RED_HOURS } from '@/lib/dispute-constants'
import { ACTIVE_SQL, TAB_SQL, disputeErrorResponse } from '@/lib/disputes-api'
import type { DisputeCounts } from '@/types/dispute'

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'

export async function GET() {
  const guard = await requirePermission('disputes:view')
  if (!guard.ok) return guard.response

  try {
    const { rows } = await db.query(`
      SELECT
        (count(*) FILTER (WHERE ${TAB_SQL.open}))::int       AS open,
        (count(*) FILTER (WHERE ${TAB_SQL.awaiting}))::int   AS awaiting,
        (count(*) FILTER (WHERE ${TAB_SQL.escalated}))::int  AS escalated,
        (count(*) FILTER (WHERE ${TAB_SQL.resolved}))::int   AS resolved,
        (count(*) FILTER (WHERE ${ACTIVE_SQL}
           AND d.created_at < now() - interval '${DISPUTE_SLA_AMBER_HOURS} hours'))::int AS overdue_48h,
        (count(*) FILTER (WHERE ${ACTIVE_SQL}
           AND d.created_at < now() - interval '${DISPUTE_SLA_RED_HOURS} hours'))::int   AS overdue_72h
      FROM disputes d
    `)
    const r = rows[0]

    // Money refunded through disputes in the last 30 days. Missing payment tables -> 0.
    let refundedAmount30d = 0
    try {
      const refunded = await db.query(`
        SELECT COALESCE(sum(r.amount), 0) AS total
        FROM refunds r
        WHERE r.status::text = 'COMPLETED'
          AND COALESCE(r.completed_at, r.created_at) >= now() - interval '30 days'
          AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM disputes d WHERE d.booking_id = r.booking_id)
      `)
      refundedAmount30d = Number(refunded.rows[0].total)
    } catch (err) {
      const code = (err as { code?: string } | null)?.code
      if (code !== '42P01' && code !== '42703') throw err
    }

    const counts: DisputeCounts = {
      open: r.open,
      awaiting: r.awaiting,
      escalated: r.escalated,
      resolved: r.resolved,
      overdue48h: r.overdue_48h,
      overdue72h: r.overdue_72h,
      refundedAmount30d,
    }
    return NextResponse.json(counts)
  } catch (err) {
    return disputeErrorResponse(err, '[GET /api/admin/disputes/counts]')
  }
}

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: app\api\admin\disputes\[disputeId]\actions\resolve\route.ts
------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// app/api/admin/disputes/[disputeId]/actions/resolve/route.ts
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'
import { requirePermission } from '@/lib/rbac-guard'
import { logAdminAction, getClientIp } from '@/lib/audit'
import { isUuid } from '@/lib/workers-api'
import {
  DISPUTE_RESOLUTIONS,
  NOTE_MAX_LENGTH,
  RESPONSE_WINDOW_HOURS,
  bookingStatusForResolution,
  isActiveDispute,
  refundPercentage,
  roundMoney,
  type DisputeResolution,
} from '@/lib/dispute-constants'
import {
  DisputeActionError,
  disputeErrorResponse,
  disputeNotFound,
  enqueueBookingEvent,
  enqueueNotification,
  lockBooking,
  setActor,
  withTx,
} from '@/lib/disputes-api'

export async function POST(
  req: NextRequest,
  { params }: { params: Promise<{ disputeId: string }> }
) {
  const guard = await requirePermission('disputes:resolve')
  if (!guard.ok) return guard.response

  const { disputeId } = await params
  if (!isUuid(disputeId)) return disputeNotFound()

  const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}))
  const resolution = String(body.resolution ?? '') as DisputeResolution
  const note = String(body.note ?? '').trim()
  const overrideWindow = body.overrideWindow === true

  if (!(DISPUTE_RESOLUTIONS as readonly string[]).includes(resolution)) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid resolution' }, { status: 400 })
  }
  if (!note) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'A resolution note is required' }, { status: 400 })
  }
  if (note.length > NOTE_MAX_LENGTH) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Note is too long (max 1000 characters)' }, { status: 400 })
  }
  if (resolution === 'PARTIAL_REFUND') {
    const a = Number(body.refundAmount)
    if (!Number.isFinite(a) || a <= 0) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Enter a refund amount above zero' }, { status: 400 })
    }
  }

  const adminId = guard.admin.adminId
  const adminEmail = guard.admin.adminEmail
  const isSuper = guard.admin.role === 'SUPER_ADMIN'

  try {
    const result = await withTx(async (client) => {
      await setActor(client, adminEmail) // booking history shows "admin:<email>"

      // Lock order: booking -> dispute (matches booking-service, avoids deadlocks).
      const pre = await client.query('SELECT booking_id FROM disputes WHERE id = $1', [disputeId])
      if (pre.rows.length === 0) throw new DisputeActionError(404, 'Dispute not found')
      const b = await lockBooking(client, pre.rows[0].booking_id)

      const { rows } = await client.query(
        `SELECT status::text AS status, worker_response, is_escalated, created_at
         FROM disputes WHERE id = $1 FOR UPDATE`,
        [disputeId]
      )
      const d = rows[0]

      if (!isActiveDispute(d.status)) {
        throw new DisputeActionError(409, 'This dispute was already resolved.')
      }
      if (d.is_escalated && !isSuper) {
        throw new DisputeActionError(
          403,
          'This dispute was escalated. Only a Super Admin can resolve it.'
        )
      }
      if (b.status !== 'DISPUTED') {
        throw new DisputeActionError(
          422,
          `The booking is ${b.status.toLowerCase().replace(/_/g, ' ')}, not disputed, so this dispute can't be resolved from here.`
        )
      }

      // Worker still has time to answer? Require an explicit override (logged).
      const dueAt = new Date(d.created_at).getTime() + RESPONSE_WINDOW_HOURS * 3_600_000
      const waitingOnWorker =
        d.status === 'OPEN' && !d.worker_response && Date.now() < dueAt
      if (waitingOnWorker && !overrideWindow) {
        throw new DisputeActionError(
          409,
          `The worker still has until ${new Date(dueAt).toLocaleString('en-GB')} to respond. Tick "resolve without waiting" to decide now.`
        )
      }

      // The server decides the money, never the browser.
      let refundAmount: number
      if (resolution === 'REFUND_GRANTED') {
        refundAmount = b.estimatedTotal
      } else if (resolution === 'NO_REFUND') {
        refundAmount = 0
      } else {
        refundAmount = roundMoney(Number(body.refundAmount))
        if (refundAmount <= 0 || refundAmount >= b.estimatedTotal) {
          throw new DisputeActionError(
            400,
            `A partial refund must be above 0 and below the booking total (${b.currency} ${b.estimatedTotal.toLocaleString()}). Use "Resolve for customer â€” 100%" for a full refund.`
          )
        }
      }
      const pct = refundPercentage(b.estimatedTotal, refundAmount)
      const newBookingStatus = bookingStatusForResolution(resolution)

      await client.query(
        `UPDATE disputes
         SET status = 'RESOLVED',
             resolution = $2::dispute_resolution,
             admin_note = $3,
             refund_amount = $4,
             resolved_by = $5,
             resolved_at = now(),
             assigned_to = COALESCE(assigned_to, $5),
             assigned_at = COALESCE(assigned_at, now()),
             updated_at = now()
         WHERE id = $1`,
        [disputeId, resolution, note, refundAmount, adminId]
      )

      await client.query(
        `UPDATE bookings
         SET status = $2::booking_status,
             refund_amount = $3,
             refund_percentage = $4,
             updated_at = now()
         WHERE id = $1`,
        [b.id, newBookingStatus, refundAmount, pct]
      )

      // payment-service consumes this and refunds / releases (payment doc, section 13).
      await enqueueBookingEvent(client, b, {
        eventType: 'booking.dispute_resolved',
        status: newBookingStatus,
        adminEmail,
        data: {
          dispute_id: disputeId,
          resolution,
          refund_amount: refundAmount,
          refund_percentage: pct,
          estimated_total: b.estimatedTotal,
          platform_fee: b.platformFee,
          worker_amount: b.workerAmount,
          currency: b.currency,
          admin_note: note,
          resolved_by_admin: true,
        },
      })

      for (const target of [
        { id: b.userId, type: 'user' as const },
        { id: b.workerId, type: 'worker' as const },
      ]) {
        await enqueueNotification(client, {
          targetId: target.id,
          targetType: target.type,
          templateKey: 'dispute_resolved',
          data: {
            booking_id: b.id,
            dispute_id: disputeId,
            resolution,
            refund_amount: refundAmount,
            currency: b.currency,
          },
          channels: ['push'],
        })
      }

      return {
        previousStatus: d.status as string,
        refundAmount,
        pct,
        bookingStatus: newBookingStatus,
        overrode: waitingOnWorker && overrideWindow,
      }
    })

    await logAdminAction({
      adminId,
      adminEmail,
      action: 'dispute.resolved',
      resourceType: 'dispute',
      resourceId: disputeId,
      oldValue: { status: result.previousStatus, bookingStatus: 'DISPUTED' },
      newValue: {
        status: 'RESOLVED',
        resolution,
        refundAmount: result.refundAmount,
        refundPercentage: result.pct,
        bookingStatus: result.bookingStatus,
        resolvedWithoutWaitingForWorker: result.overrode,
      },
      notes: note,
      ip: getClientIp(req),
      userAgent: req.headers.get('user-agent') ?? undefined,
    })

    return NextResponse.json({
      ok: true,
      resolution,
      refundAmount: result.refundAmount,
      refundPercentage: result.pct,
      bookingStatus: result.bookingStatus,
    })
  } catch (err) {
    return disputeErrorResponse(err, '[POST dispute resolve]')
  }
}

FILE: app\api\admin\disputes\[disputeId]\notes\route.ts
------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// app/api/admin/disputes/[disputeId]/notes/route.ts
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'
import { db } from '@/lib/db'
import { requirePermission } from '@/lib/rbac-guard'
import { logAdminAction, getClientIp } from '@/lib/audit'
import { isUuid } from '@/lib/workers-api'
import { NOTE_MAX_LENGTH } from '@/lib/dispute-constants'
import { disputeErrorResponse, disputeNotFound } from '@/lib/disputes-api'

export async function GET(
  req: NextRequest,
  { params }: { params: Promise<{ disputeId: string }> }
) {
  const guard = await requirePermission('disputes:view')
  if (!guard.ok) return guard.response

  const { disputeId } = await params
  if (!isUuid(disputeId)) return disputeNotFound()

  try {
    const { rows } = await db.query(
      `SELECT id, admin_email, note, created_at
       FROM dispute_admin_notes
       WHERE dispute_id = $1
       ORDER BY created_at DESC
       LIMIT 100`,
      [disputeId]
    )
    return NextResponse.json(
      rows.map((r) => ({
        id: r.id,
        adminEmail: r.admin_email,
        note: r.note,
        createdAt: r.created_at,
      }))
    )
  } catch (err) {
    return disputeErrorResponse(err, '[GET dispute notes]')
  }
}

export async function POST(
  req: NextRequest,
  { params }: { params: Promise<{ disputeId: string }> }
) {
  const guard = await requirePermission('disputes:note')
  if (!guard.ok) return guard.response

  const { disputeId } = await params
  if (!isUuid(disputeId)) return disputeNotFound()

  const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}))
  const note = String(body.note ?? '').trim()
  if (!note) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'A note is required' }, { status: 400 })
  if (note.length > 2000) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Notes are limited to 2000 characters' }, { status: 400 })
  }

  try {
    const exists = await db.query('SELECT 1 FROM disputes WHERE id = $1', [disputeId])
    if (exists.rows.length === 0) return disputeNotFound()

    const { rows } = await db.query(
      `INSERT INTO dispute_admin_notes (dispute_id, admin_id, admin_email, note)
       VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4)
       RETURNING id, admin_email, note, created_at`,
      [disputeId, guard.admin.adminId, guard.admin.adminEmail, note]
    )

    await logAdminAction({
      adminId: guard.admin.adminId,
      adminEmail: guard.admin.adminEmail,
      action: 'dispute.note_added',
      resourceType: 'dispute',
      resourceId: disputeId,
      notes: note.slice(0, NOTE_MAX_LENGTH),
      ip: getClientIp(req),
      userAgent: req.headers.get('user-agent') ?? undefined,
    })

    const r = rows[0]
    return NextResponse.json(
      { id: r.id, adminEmail: r.admin_email, note: r.note, createdAt: r.created_at },
      { status: 201 }
    )
  } catch (err) {
    return disputeErrorResponse(err, '[POST dispute note]')
  }
}

FILE: db\migrations\010_disputes_admin.sql
