-- 010_disputes_admin.sql
-- Disputes pages: assignment + escalation columns, internal notes, list indexes,
-- and a get_needs_attention() that counts EVERY unresolved dispute.
--
-- NOTE: `disputes` is owned by booking-service. Everything here is additive
-- (IF NOT EXISTS), same as 006, 007 and 008. Tell the booking-service team
-- (see section 9 "Backend contract").

-- ---------------------------------------------------------
-- 1. Assignment + escalation (the status enum is NOT touched)
-- ---------------------------------------------------------
ALTER TABLE public.disputes
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS assigned_to     uuid NULL REFERENCES admin_users(id),
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS assigned_at     timestamptz NULL,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS is_escalated    boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS escalated_at    timestamptz NULL,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS escalated_by    uuid NULL REFERENCES admin_users(id),
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS escalation_note text NULL;

-- Index predicates use enum literals (not ::text) because enum->text is not IMMUTABLE.
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_disputes_created_at
  ON public.disputes (created_at DESC);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_disputes_assigned_to
  ON public.disputes (assigned_to) WHERE assigned_to IS NOT NULL;

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_disputes_escalated
  ON public.disputes (created_at)
  WHERE is_escalated AND status NOT IN ('RESOLVED', 'CLOSED');

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_disputes_resolved_at
  ON public.disputes (resolved_at DESC) WHERE resolved_at IS NOT NULL;

-- Refund tab joins refunds -> disputes on booking_id
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_refunds_booking_initiated
  ON public.refunds (booking_id, initiated_at DESC);

-- ---------------------------------------------------------
-- 2. Internal dispute notes (never visible to customer or worker)
-- ---------------------------------------------------------
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.dispute_admin_notes (
  id          uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  dispute_id  uuid NOT NULL,                       -- no FK (cross-service table)
  admin_id    uuid NULL REFERENCES admin_users(id),
  admin_email varchar(255) NOT NULL,
  note        text NOT NULL,
  created_at  timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_dispute_admin_notes_dispute
  ON public.dispute_admin_notes (dispute_id, created_at DESC);

-- ---------------------------------------------------------
-- 3. get_needs_attention() â€” dashboard "unresolved disputes older than 24h"
--    Copied from 002_dashboard_functions.sql. ONLY the staleDisputes block changed:
--    it used to count status = 'OPEN' only, so disputes the worker had already
--    answered (WORKER_RESPONDED) or that were UNDER_REVIEW disappeared from
--    the dashboard even though nobody had decided them.
--    If you edited this function after 002, re-apply just that one block.
-- ---------------------------------------------------------
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION get_needs_attention()
RETURNS JSON AS $$
DECLARE
  v_result JSON;
BEGIN
  SELECT json_build_object(
    'staleDisputes', (
      SELECT COALESCE(json_agg(json_build_object(
        'id', d.id, 'bookingId', d.booking_id,
        'ageHours', EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (NOW() - d.created_at)) / 3600
      )), '[]')
      FROM disputes d
      WHERE d.status::text IN ('OPEN', 'WORKER_RESPONDED', 'UNDER_REVIEW')
        AND d.created_at < NOW() - INTERVAL '24 hours'
    ),
    'staleVerifications', (
      SELECT COALESCE(json_agg(json_build_object(
        'id', w.id, 'name', w.name,
        'category', (
          SELECT c.name FROM worker_skills ws
          JOIN categories c ON c.id = ws.category_id
          WHERE ws.worker_id = w.id
          ORDER BY ws.created_at
          LIMIT 1
        ),
        'ageHours', EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (NOW() - w.created_at)) / 3600
      )), '[]')
      FROM workers w
      WHERE w.verification_status = 'PENDING_REVIEW' AND w.created_at < NOW() - INTERVAL '48 hours'
    ),
    'failedPayments', (
      SELECT COALESCE(json_agg(json_build_object(
        'id', p.id, 'bookingId', p.booking_id, 'amount', p.booking_amount
      )), '[]')
      FROM payments p
      WHERE p.status = 'FAILED'
    ),
    'stuckPendingBookings', (
      SELECT COALESCE(json_agg(json_build_object(
        'id', b.id, 'city', w.city, 'category', b.category_id,
        'ageMinutes', EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (NOW() - b.created_at)) / 60
      )), '[]')
      FROM bookings b
      LEFT JOIN workers w ON w.id = b.worker_id
      WHERE b.status = 'PENDING' AND b.created_at < NOW() - INTERVAL '30 minutes'
    ),
    'flaggedReviews', '[]'::json
  ) INTO v_result;

  RETURN v_result;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE;

// components/workers/WorkersTableSection.tsx
'use client'

import { useMemo } from 'react'
import {
  buildWorkerExportQuery,
  useWorkerFilterOptions,
  useWorkerFilters,
  useWorkersList,
} from '@/hooks/useWorkers'
import WorkerFilterBar from './WorkerFilterBar'
import WorkerTable from './WorkerTable'

const KNOWN_CITIES = ['Lahore', 'Karachi', 'Islamabad', 'Rawalpindi']

export default function WorkersTableSection() {
  const { filters, update, reset } = useWorkerFilters()
  const { workers, isLoading, error, mutate } = useWorkersList(filters)
  const { categories } = useWorkerFilterOptions()

  const totalPages = workers?.totalPages ?? 1

  const pageSummary = useMemo(() => {
    if (!workers) return ''
    const start = (workers.page - 1) * workers.pageSize + 1
    const end = Math.min(workers.page * workers.pageSize, workers.total)
    return `${start}â€“${end} of ${workers.total}`
  }, [workers])

  const exportHref = `/api/admin/workers/export?${buildWorkerExportQuery(filters)}`

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-gray-500">
          Workers {workers ? `(${workers.total.toLocaleString()})` : ''}
        </h2>
        <a
          href={exportHref}
          className="flex h-9 items-center rounded-md border border-gray-300 bg-white px-3 text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-50"
        >
          Export CSV
        </a>
      </div>

      <WorkerFilterBar
        filters={filters}
        onChange={update}
        onReset={reset}
        cities={KNOWN_CITIES}
        categories={categories}
      />

      <WorkerTable
        rows={workers?.data ?? []}
        isLoading={isLoading}
        error={error}
        onRetry={() => mutate()}
      />

      {workers && workers.total > 0 && (
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-sm text-gray-500">
          <span>{pageSummary}</span>
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <button
              className="h-9 rounded-md border border-gray-300 px-3 disabled:opacity-40"
              disabled={filters.page <= 1}
              onClick={() => update({ page: filters.page - 1 })}
            >
              Previous
            </button>
            <button
              className="h-9 rounded-md border border-gray-300 px-3 disabled:opacity-40"
              disabled={filters.page >= totalPages}
              onClick={() => update({ page: filters.page + 1 })}
            >
              Next
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

// components/workers/WorkerFilterBar.tsx
'use client'

import { Input } from '@/components/ui/Input'
import type { CategoryOption, WorkerFilters } from '@/types/worker'

interface Props {
  filters: WorkerFilters
  onChange: (patch: Partial<WorkerFilters>) => void
  onReset: () => void
  cities: string[]
  categories: CategoryOption[]
}

const SELECT_CLASS = 'h-9 rounded-md border border-gray-300 px-3 text-sm'

function Field({ label, children }: { label: string; children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <div>
      <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-medium text-gray-700">{label}</label>
      {children}
    </div>
  )
}

export default function WorkerFilterBar({ filters, onChange, onReset, cities, categories }: Props) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-white p-3">
      <div className="min-w-[220px] flex-1">
        <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-medium text-gray-700">Search</label>
        <Input
          placeholder="Name, phone, or email"
          value={filters.search}
          onChange={(e) => onChange({ search: e.target.value })}
        />
      </div>

        <select
          className={SELECT_CLASS}
          value={filters.city}
          onChange={(e) => onChange({ city: e.target.value })}

            <option key={c} value={c}>{c}</option>
          ))}

          <option value="all">All categories</option>

            <option key={c.id} value={c.id}>{c.name}</option>
          ))}
        </select>
      </Field>

      <Field label="Verification">

          <option value="unverified">Unverified</option>

          <option value="suspended">Suspended</option>
        </select>
      </Field>

      <Field label="Status">
        <select
          className={SELECT_CLASS}
          value={filters.status}
          onChange={(e) => onChange({ status: e.target.value as WorkerFilters['status'] })}
        >
          <option value="all">All</option>
          <option value="active">Active</option>
          <option value="inactive">Inactive</option>
          <option value="suspended">Suspended</option>
          <option value="banned">Banned</option>
          <option value="deleted">Deleted</option>
        </select>
      </Field>

      <Field label="Presence">
        <select
          className={SELECT_CLASS}
          value={filters.presence}
          onChange={(e) => onChange({ presence: e.target.value as WorkerFilters['presence'] })}
        >
          <option value="all">Any</option>
          <option value="online">Online</option>
          <option value="away">Away</option>
          <option value="on_job">On job</option>
          <option value="paused">Paused</option>
          <option value="offline">Offline</option>
        </select>
      </Field>

      <Field label="Rating">
        <select
          className={SELECT_CLASS}
          value={filters.ratingBucket}
          onChange={(e) => onChange({ ratingBucket: e.target.value as WorkerFilters['ratingBucket'] })}
        >
          <option value="all">Any</option>
          <option value="lt3">Below 3.0 (needs review)</option>
          <option value="3-4">3.0 â€“ 3.9</option>
          <option value="4+">4.0 +</option>
          <option value="unrated">No reviews yet</option>
        </select>
      </Field>

      <Field label="From">
        <Input
          type="date"
          value={filters.dateFrom ?? ''}
          onChange={(e) => onChange({ dateFrom: e.target.value || null })}
        />
      </Field>
      <Field label="To">
        <Input
          type="date"
          value={filters.dateTo ?? ''}
          onChange={(e) => onChange({ dateTo: e.target.value || null })}
        />
      </Field>

      <button onClick={onReset} className="h-9 px-3 text-sm text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-700">
        Clear filters
      </button>
    </div>
  )
}

// components/workers/WorkerTable.tsx
'use client'

import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'
import Image from 'next/image'
import { Star } from 'lucide-react'
import DataTable, { DataTableColumn } from '@/components/shared/DataTable'
import { StatusBadge } from '@/components/shared/StatusBadge'
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/Badge'
import {
  PRESENCE_LABEL,
  PRESENCE_TONE,
  STATUS_LABEL,
  STATUS_VARIANT,
  VERIFICATION_LABEL,
  VERIFICATION_VARIANT,
  formatDate,
  initial,
} from './worker-ui'
import type { WorkerListItem } from '@/types/worker'

interface Props {
  rows: WorkerListItem[]
  isLoading: boolean
  error: unknown
  onRetry: () => void
}

export default function WorkerTable({ rows, isLoading, error, onRetry }: Props) {
  const router = useRouter()

  const columns: DataTableColumn<WorkerListItem>[] = [
    {
      key: 'name',
      header: 'Name',
      render: (w) => (
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          {w.avatarUrl ? (
            <Image
              src={w.avatarUrl}
              alt=""
              width={24}
              height={24}
              unoptimized
              className="h-6 w-6 rounded-full object-cover"
            />
          ) : (
            <div className="h-6 w-6 rounded-full bg-gray-200 text-center text-[10px] leading-6 text-gray-500">
              {initial(w.name)}
            </div>
          )}
          <span className="font-medium text-gray-900">{w.name ?? 'Unnamed'}</span>
          {w.isFeatured && (
            <Star size={12} className="fill-[#D97706] text-[#D97706]" aria-label="Featured" />
          )}
        </div>
      ),
    },
    { key: 'phone', header: 'Phone', render: (w) => w.phone ?? 'â€”', className: 'font-mono text-xs' },
    { key: 'city', header: 'City', render: (w) => w.city ?? 'â€”' },
    {
      key: 'category',
      header: 'Category',
      render: (w) => w.categories ?? 'â€”',
      className: 'max-w-[180px] truncate',
    },
    {
      key: 'rating',
      header: 'Rating',
      render: (w) =>
        w.totalReviews > 0 ? (
          <span className={w.avgRating < 3 ? 'font-medium text-[#D97706]' : ''}>
            â˜… {w.avgRating.toFixed(1)}{' '}
            <span className="text-xs text-gray-400">({w.totalReviews})</span>
          </span>
        ) : (
          <span className="text-gray-400">New</span>
        ),
    },
    { key: 'jobs', header: 'Jobs', render: (w) => w.totalBookings },
    {
      key: 'verification',
      header: 'Verified',
      render: (w) => (
        <StatusBadge
          status={w.verificationStatus}
          variant={VERIFICATION_VARIANT[w.verificationStatus] ?? 'neutral'}
          label={VERIFICATION_LABEL[w.verificationStatus] ?? w.verificationStatus}
        />
      ),
    },
    {
      key: 'presence',
      header: 'Presence',
      render: (w) => (
        <Badge tone={PRESENCE_TONE[w.presence] ?? 'neutral'}>
          {PRESENCE_LABEL[w.presence] ?? w.presence}
        </Badge>
      ),
    },
    {
      key: 'status',
      header: 'Status',
      render: (w) => (
        <StatusBadge
          status={w.status}
          variant={STATUS_VARIANT[w.status] ?? 'neutral'}
          label={STATUS_LABEL[w.status] ?? w.status}
        />
      ),
    },
    { key: 'joined', header: 'Joined', render: (w) => formatDate(w.createdAt) },
  ]

  return (
    <DataTable
      columns={columns}
      rows={rows}
      rowKey={(w) => w.id}
      isLoading={isLoading}
      error={error}
      onRetry={onRetry}
      emptyMessage="No workers match these filters."
      onRowClick={(w) => router.push(`/workers/${w.id}`)}
    />
  )
}

// components/workers/WorkerKpiGrid.tsx
'use client'

import { KpiCard, KpiCardSkeleton } from '@/components/dashboard/KpiCard'
import { useWorkerKpis } from '@/hooks/useWorkers'

export function WorkerKpiGrid() {
  const { kpis, error, isLoading, mutate } = useWorkerKpis()

  if (error) {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-lg border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-4 text-sm text-red-600">
        Couldn't load worker stats.{' '}
        <button onClick={() => mutate()} className="underline">
          Retry
        </button>
      </div>
    )
  }

  if (isLoading || !kpis) {
    return (
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
        {Array.from({ length: 4 }).map((_, i) => (
          <KpiCardSkeleton key={i} />
        ))}
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
      <KpiCard
        label="Total Workers"
        value={kpis.totalWorkers.toLocaleString('en-PK')}
        color="#1F6F5F"
      />
      <KpiCard
        label="Signups Today"
        value={kpis.signupsToday.toString()}
        changePct={kpis.signupsTodayChangePct}
        sparkline={kpis.signupsSparkline7d}
        color="#2563EB"
      />
      <KpiCard
        label="Online Now"
        value={kpis.onlineNow.toLocaleString('en-PK')}
        color="#D97706"
      />
      <KpiCard
        label="Suspended / Banned"
        value={kpis.suspendedOrBanned.toLocaleString('en-PK')}
        color="#DC2626"
      />
    </div>
  )
}

// components/workers/worker-ui.ts
// Labels, colours and formatters shared by every worker component.
import { formatDistanceToNow } from 'date-fns'
import type { BadgeTone } from '@/components/ui/Badge'
import type {
  WorkerBadgeKey,
  WorkerPresence,
  WorkerStatus,
  WorkerVerificationStatus,
} from '@/types/worker'

export type StatusVariant = 'success' | 'warning' | 'danger' | 'neutral'

export const STATUS_VARIANT: Record<WorkerStatus, StatusVariant> = {
  active: 'success',
  inactive: 'neutral',
  suspended: 'warning',
  banned: 'danger',
  deleted: 'danger',
}

export const STATUS_LABEL: Record<WorkerStatus, string> = {
  active: 'Active',
  inactive: 'Inactive',
  suspended: 'Suspended',
  banned: 'Banned',
  deleted: 'Deleted',
}

export const VERIFICATION_VARIANT: Record<WorkerVerificationStatus, StatusVariant> = {
  verified: 'success',
  pending_review: 'warning',
  unverified: 'neutral',
  rejected: 'danger',
  suspended: 'danger',
}

export const VERIFICATION_LABEL: Record<WorkerVerificationStatus, string> = {
  verified: 'Verified',
  pending_review: 'Pending review',
  unverified: 'Unverified',
  rejected: 'Rejected',
  suspended: 'Suspended',
}

export const PRESENCE_TONE: Record<WorkerPresence, BadgeTone> = {
  online: 'success',
  away: 'warning',
  offline: 'neutral',
  paused: 'danger',
  on_job: 'info',
}

export const PRESENCE_LABEL: Record<WorkerPresence, string> = {
  online: 'Online',
  away: 'Away',
  offline: 'Offline',
  paused: 'Paused',
  on_job: 'On job',
}

export const BADGE_LABEL: Record<WorkerBadgeKey, string> = {
  top_rated: 'Top Rated',
  background_verified: 'Background Verified',
}

export const DAY_NAMES = [
  'Sunday', 'Monday', 'Tuesday', 'Wednesday', 'Thursday', 'Friday', 'Saturday',
]

export function formatDate(iso: string | null | undefined): string {
  if (!iso) return 'â€”'
  return new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString('en-GB', {
    day: '2-digit',
    month: 'short',
    year: 'numeric',
  })
}

export function formatPkr(amount: number | null | undefined, currency = 'PKR'): string {
  if (amount === null || amount === undefined) return 'â€”'
  return `${currency} ${amount.toLocaleString()}`
}

export function timeAgo(iso: string | null | undefined): string {
  if (!iso) return 'never'
  return formatDistanceToNow(new Date(iso), { addSuffix: true })
}

export function initial(name: string | null | undefined): string {
  return (name ?? '?').charAt(0).toUpperCase()
}

// components/workers/WorkerAdminActions.tsx
'use client'

import { useState } from 'react'
import { toast } from 'sonner'
import { Card } from '@/components/ui/Card'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/Button'
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/Input'
import ConfirmDialog from '@/components/shared/ConfirmDialog'
import { usePermission } from '@/hooks/usePermission'
import { workerActions } from '@/hooks/useWorkers'
import { BADGE_LABEL } from './worker-ui'
import type { WorkerBadgeKey, WorkerDetail } from '@/types/worker'

type DialogKind = 'warn' | 'suspend' | 'ban' | 'edit' | 'reverify' | 'offline' | null

const BADGE_KEYS: WorkerBadgeKey[] = ['top_rated', 'background_verified']

export default function WorkerAdminActions({
  worker,
  onChanged,
}: {
  worker: WorkerDetail
  onChanged: () => void
}) {
  const [dialog, setDialog] = useState<DialogKind>(null)
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [suspendDuration, setSuspendDuration] = useState<'7d' | '30d' | 'custom'>('7d')
  const [suspendUntil, setSuspendUntil] = useState('')
  const [editPhone, setEditPhone] = useState('')
  const [editEmail, setEditEmail] = useState('')

  const canWarn = usePermission('workers:warn')
  const canSuspend = usePermission('workers:suspend')
  const canBan = usePermission('workers:ban')
  const canEdit = usePermission('workers:edit')
  const canFeature = usePermission('workers:feature')
  const canBadge = usePermission('workers:badge')
  const canForceOffline = usePermission('workers:force-offline')
  const canVerify = usePermission('workers:verify')

  const name = worker.name?.trim() || 'this worker'
  const isLive = ['online', 'away', 'on_job'].includes(worker.presence)

  async function run(key: string, fn: () => Promise<unknown>, success: string) {
    setBusy(key)
    try {
      await fn()
      toast.success(success)
      onChanged()
    } catch (e: any) {
      toast.error(e.message)
    } finally {
      setBusy(null)
    }
  }

  function openEdit() {
    setEditPhone(worker.phone ?? '')
    setEditEmail(worker.email ?? '')
    setDialog('edit')
  }

  const hasAnyAction =
    canWarn || canSuspend || canBan || canEdit || canFeature || canBadge || canForceOffline || canVerify

  return (
    <Card className="p-4">
      <h2 className="mb-3 text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-gray-500">Admin Actions</h2>

      {!hasAnyAction && (
        <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">You have view-only access to workers.</p>
      )}

      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
        {canWarn && (
          <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => setDialog('warn')}>Send warning</Button>
        )}

        {canSuspend && worker.status !== 'suspended' && worker.status !== 'banned' && (
          <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => setDialog('suspend')}>Suspend</Button>
        )}
        {canSuspend && worker.status === 'suspended' && (
          <Button
            variant="secondary"
            loading={busy === 'unsuspend'}
            onClick={() =>
              run('unsuspend', () => workerActions.unsuspend(worker.id), 'Suspension lifted')
            }
          >
            Lift suspension
          </Button>
        )}

        {canBan && worker.status !== 'banned' && (
          <Button variant="destructive" onClick={() => setDialog('ban')}>Ban permanently</Button>
        )}

        {canEdit && (
          <Button variant="secondary" onClick={openEdit}>Edit phone/email</Button>
        )}

        {canForceOffline && isLive && (
          <Button
            variant="secondary"
            onClick={() => setDialog('offline')}
            title="Pauses the worker and marks them offline"
          >
            Force offline
          </Button>
        )}

        {canVerify && worker.verificationStatus === 'verified' && (
          <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => setDialog('reverify')}>
            Request re-verification
          </Button>
        )}

        {canFeature && (
          <Button
            variant="secondary"
            loading={busy === 'feature'}
            onClick={() =>
              run(
                'feature',
                () => workerActions.setFeatured(worker.id, !worker.isFeatured),
                worker.isFeatured ? 'Removed from featured' : 'Worker featured'
              )
            }
          >
            {worker.isFeatured ? 'Remove featured' : 'Feature worker'}
          </Button>
        )}
      </div>

      {canBadge && (
        <div className="mt-4 border-t border-gray-100 pt-3">
          <p className="mb-2 text-xs font-medium text-gray-700">Badges</p>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            {BADGE_KEYS.map((key) => {
              const has = worker.badges.includes(key)
              return (
                <Button
                  key={key}
                  size="sm"
                  variant={has ? 'primary' : 'secondary'}
                  loading={busy === `badge:${key}`}
                  onClick={() =>
                    run(
                      `badge:${key}`,
                      () => workerActions.setBadge(worker.id, key, has ? 'remove' : 'add'),
                      has ? `${BADGE_LABEL[key]} removed` : `${BADGE_LABEL[key]} awarded`
                    )
                  }
                >
                  {has ? `âœ“ ${BADGE_LABEL[key]}` : `Award ${BADGE_LABEL[key]}`}
                </Button>
              )
            })}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Warn */}
      <ConfirmDialog
        open={dialog === 'warn'}
        title={`Warn ${name}?`}
        description="This increments their warning count and is visible in their audit trail."
        requireReason
        confirmLabel="Send warning"
        destructive={false}
        onClose={() => setDialog(null)}
        onConfirm={async (reason) => {
          await workerActions.warn(worker.id, reason!)
          toast.success('Warning sent')
          onChanged()
        }}
      />

      {/* Suspend: the duration picker lives inside the dialog via the children slot */}
      <ConfirmDialog
        open={dialog === 'suspend'}
        title={`Suspend ${name}?`}
        description="They will be removed from search and can't accept bookings while suspended."
        requireReason
        confirmLabel="Suspend"
        onClose={() => setDialog(null)}
        onConfirm={async (reason) => {
          if (suspendDuration === 'custom' && !suspendUntil) {
            throw new Error('Pick an end date and time')
          }
          await workerActions.suspend(
            worker.id,
            reason!,
            suspendDuration,
            suspendDuration === 'custom' ? new Date(suspendUntil).toISOString() : undefined
          )
          toast.success('Worker suspended')
          onChanged()
        }}
      >
        <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-medium text-gray-700">Duration</label>
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          {(['7d', '30d', 'custom'] as const).map((d) => (
            <button
              key={d}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setSuspendDuration(d)}
              className={`h-8 rounded-md border px-3 text-xs ${
                suspendDuration === d
                  ? 'border-[#1F6F5F] text-[#1F6F5F]'
                  : 'border-gray-300 text-gray-600'
              }`}
            >
              {d === '7d' ? '7 days' : d === '30d' ? '30 days' : 'Custom'}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
        {suspendDuration === 'custom' && (
          <Input
            type="datetime-local"
            className="mt-2"
            value={suspendUntil}
            onChange={(e) => setSuspendUntil(e.target.value)}
          />
        )}
      </ConfirmDialog>

      {/* Ban */}
      <ConfirmDialog
        open={dialog === 'ban'}
        title={`Ban ${name} permanently?`}
        description="This is a serious, hard-to-reverse action. The worker is removed from search and can't accept bookings."
        requireReason
        requireTypedConfirmation={worker.name?.trim() || 'BAN'}
        confirmLabel="Ban permanently"
        onClose={() => setDialog(null)}
        onConfirm={async (reason) => {
          await workerActions.ban(worker.id, reason!)
          toast.success('Worker banned')
          onChanged()
        }}
      />

      {/* Force offline */}
      <ConfirmDialog
        open={dialog === 'offline'}
        title={`Force ${name} offline?`}
        description="Sets them to paused and marks them offline. They can resume from the app."
        requireReason
        confirmLabel="Force offline"
        onClose={() => setDialog(null)}
        onConfirm={async (reason) => {
          await workerActions.forceOffline(worker.id, reason!)
          toast.success('Worker forced offline')
          onChanged()
        }}
      />

      {/* Request re-verification */}
      <ConfirmDialog
        open={dialog === 'reverify'}
        title={`Request re-verification from ${name}?`}
        description="They go back to Unverified and leave search results until they re-submit documents."
        requireReason
        confirmLabel="Request re-verification"
        destructive={false}
        onClose={() => setDialog(null)}
        onConfirm={async (reason) => {
          await workerActions.requestReverification(worker.id, reason!)
          toast.success('Re-verification requested')
          onChanged()
        }}
      />

      {/* Edit contact */}
      {dialog === 'edit' && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/40 px-4">
          <div className="w-full max-w-md rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-gray-900">Edit contact info</h2>
            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-600">For support cases where the worker is locked out.</p>
            <div className="mt-4 flex flex-col gap-3">
              <div>
                <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-medium text-gray-700">Phone</label>
                <Input value={editPhone} onChange={(e) => setEditPhone(e.target.value)} />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-medium text-gray-700">Email</label>
                <Input value={editEmail} onChange={(e) => setEditEmail(e.target.value)} />
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="mt-5 flex justify-end gap-2">
              <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => setDialog(null)}>Cancel</Button>
              <Button
                loading={busy === 'edit'}
                onClick={async () => {
                  setBusy('edit')
                  try {
                    await workerActions.editContact(worker.id, editPhone, editEmail)
                    toast.success('Contact info updated')
                    setDialog(null)
                    onChanged()
                  } catch (e: any) {
                    toast.error(e.message)
                  } finally {
                    setBusy(null)
                  }
                }}
              >
                Save
              </Button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </Card>
  )
}

'use client'

import { useMemo } from 'react'
import { useUsersList, useUserFilters } from '@/hooks/useUsers'
import UserFilterBar from './UserFilterBar'
import UserTable from './UserTable'
import type { UserFilters } from '@/types/user'

const KNOWN_CITIES = ['Lahore', 'Karachi', 'Islamabad', 'Rawalpindi']

function buildExportQuery(filters: UserFilters): string {
  const qs = new URLSearchParams()
  if (filters.search) qs.set('search', filters.search)
  if (filters.city !== 'all') qs.set('city', filters.city)
  if (filters.status !== 'all') qs.set('status', filters.status)
  if (filters.bookingsBucket !== 'all') qs.set('bookingsBucket', filters.bookingsBucket)
  if (filters.dateFrom) qs.set('dateFrom', filters.dateFrom)
  if (filters.dateTo) qs.set('dateTo', filters.dateTo)
  return qs.toString()
}

export default function UsersTableSection() {
  const { filters, update, reset } = useUserFilters()
  const { users, isLoading, error, mutate } = useUsersList(filters)

  const totalPages = users?.totalPages ?? 1

  const pageSummary = useMemo(() => {
    if (!users) return ''
    const start = (users.page - 1) * users.pageSize + 1
    const end = Math.min(users.page * users.pageSize, users.total)
    return `${start}â€“${end} of ${users.total}`
  }, [users])

  const exportHref = `/api/admin/users/export?${buildExportQuery(filters)}`

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-gray-500">
          Users {users ? `(${users.total.toLocaleString()})` : ''}
        </h2>
        <a
          href={exportHref}
          className="flex h-9 items-center rounded-md border border-gray-300 bg-white px-3 text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-50"
        >
          Export CSV
        </a>
      </div>

      <UserFilterBar filters={filters} onChange={update} onReset={reset} cities={KNOWN_CITIES} />

      <UserTable
        rows={users?.data ?? []}
        isLoading={isLoading}
        error={error}
        onRetry={() => mutate()}
      />

      {users && users.total > 0 && (
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-sm text-gray-500">
          <span>{pageSummary}</span>
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <button
              className="h-9 rounded-md border border-gray-300 px-3 disabled:opacity-40"
              disabled={filters.page <= 1}
              onClick={() => update({ page: filters.page - 1 })}
            >
              Previous
            </button>
            <button
              className="h-9 rounded-md border border-gray-300 px-3 disabled:opacity-40"
              disabled={filters.page >= totalPages}
              onClick={() => update({ page: filters.page + 1 })}
            >
              Next
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

          <option value="inactive">Inactive</option>
          <option value="suspended">Suspended</option>

        >
          <option value="all">Any</option>
          <option value="0">0</option>
          <option value="1-5">1 â€“ 5</option>
          <option value="5-20">5 â€“ 20</option>
          <option value="20+">20+</option>
        </select>
      </div>

      <div>
        <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-medium text-gray-700">From</label>
        <Input
          type="date"

        />
      </div>
      <div>
        <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-medium text-gray-700">To</label>
        <Input
          type="date"
          value={filters.dateTo ?? ''}

        />
      </div>

      </button>

// components/users/UserTable.tsx
'use client'

import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'
import Image from 'next/image'
import DataTable, { DataTableColumn } from '@/components/shared/DataTable'
import { StatusBadge } from '@/components/shared/StatusBadge'
import type { UserListItem } from '@/types/user'

const STATUS_VARIANT: Record<string, 'success' | 'warning' | 'danger' | 'neutral'> = {
  active: 'success',
  inactive: 'neutral',
  suspended: 'warning',
  banned: 'danger',
  deleted: 'danger',
}

interface Props {
  rows: UserListItem[]
  isLoading: boolean
  error: unknown
  onRetry: () => void
}

export default function UserTable({ rows, isLoading, error, onRetry }: Props) {
  const router = useRouter()

  const columns: DataTableColumn<UserListItem>[] = [
    {
      key: 'name',
      header: 'Name',
      render: (u) => (
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          {u.avatarUrl ? (
            <Image src={u.avatarUrl} alt="" width={24} height={24} className="rounded-full" />
          ) : (
            <div className="h-6 w-6 rounded-full bg-gray-200 text-center text-[10px] leading-6 text-gray-500">
              {(u.name ?? '?').charAt(0).toUpperCase()}
            </div>
          )}
          <span className="font-medium text-gray-900">{u.name ?? 'Unnamed'}</span>
        </div>
      ),
    },
    { key: 'phone', header: 'Phone', render: (u) => u.phone ?? 'â€”', className: 'font-mono text-xs' },
    { key: 'city', header: 'City', render: (u) => u.city ?? 'â€”' },
    { key: 'bookings', header: 'Bookings', render: (u) => u.totalBookings },
    {
      key: 'joined',
      header: 'Joined',
      render: (u) => new Date(u.createdAt).toLocaleDateString('en-GB', { day: '2-digit', month: 'short', year: 'numeric' }),
    },
    {
      key: 'status',
      header: 'Status',
      render: (u) => <StatusBadge status={u.status} variant={STATUS_VARIANT[u.status] ?? 'neutral'} />,
    },
  ]

  return (
    <DataTable
      columns={columns}
      rows={rows}
      rowKey={(u) => u.id}
      isLoading={isLoading}
      error={error}
      onRetry={onRetry}
      emptyMessage="No users match these filters."
      onRowClick={(u) => router.push(`/users/${u.id}`)}
    />
  )
}

// hooks/useWorkers.ts
import useSWR from 'swr'
import { useState } from 'react'
import { fetcher } from '@/lib/swr-fetcher'
import type {
  AvailabilityAwareList,
  CategoryOption,
  WorkerAvailabilityData,
  WorkerBadgeKey,
  WorkerCatalog,
  WorkerDetail,
  WorkerDocumentRow,
  WorkerEarnings,
  WorkerFilters,
  WorkerJobRow,
  WorkerKpis,
  WorkerListResponse,
  WorkerPerformance,
  WorkerReviewRow,
} from '@/types/worker'

export const DEFAULT_WORKER_FILTERS: WorkerFilters = {
  search: '',
  city: 'all',
  categoryId: 'all',
  verification: 'all',
  status: 'all',
  presence: 'all',
  ratingBucket: 'all',
  dateFrom: null,
  dateTo: null,
  sortBy: 'created_at',
  sortDir: 'desc',
  page: 1,
  pageSize: 20,
}

function filterParams(filters: WorkerFilters): URLSearchParams {
  const qs = new URLSearchParams()
  if (filters.search) qs.set('search', filters.search)
  if (filters.city !== 'all') qs.set('city', filters.city)
  if (filters.categoryId !== 'all') qs.set('categoryId', filters.categoryId)
  if (filters.verification !== 'all') qs.set('verification', filters.verification)
  if (filters.status !== 'all') qs.set('status', filters.status)
  if (filters.presence !== 'all') qs.set('presence', filters.presence)
  if (filters.ratingBucket !== 'all') qs.set('ratingBucket', filters.ratingBucket)
  if (filters.dateFrom) qs.set('dateFrom', filters.dateFrom)
  if (filters.dateTo) qs.set('dateTo', filters.dateTo)
  return qs
}

function buildQuery(filters: WorkerFilters) {
  const qs = filterParams(filters)
  qs.set('sortBy', filters.sortBy)
  qs.set('sortDir', filters.sortDir)
  qs.set('page', String(filters.page))
  qs.set('pageSize', String(filters.pageSize))
  return qs.toString()
}

/** Query string for the CSV export: filters only, no paging/sorting. */
export function buildWorkerExportQuery(filters: WorkerFilters): string {
  return filterParams(filters).toString()
}

export function useWorkerKpis() {
  const { data, error, isLoading, mutate } = useSWR<WorkerKpis>(
    '/api/admin/workers/kpis',
    fetcher,
    { refreshInterval: 30_000 } // "Online now" is live data
  )
  return { kpis: data, isLoading, error, mutate }
}

export function useWorkerFilterOptions() {
  const { data } = useSWR<{ categories: CategoryOption[] }>(
    '/api/admin/workers/filter-options',
    fetcher
  )
  return { categories: data?.categories ?? [] }
}

export function useWorkersList(filters: WorkerFilters) {
  const { data, error, isLoading, mutate } = useSWR<WorkerListResponse>(
    `/api/admin/workers?${buildQuery(filters)}`,
    fetcher,
    { keepPreviousData: true }
  )
  return { workers: data, isLoading, error, mutate }
}

export function useWorkerFilters(initial: Partial<WorkerFilters> = {}) {
  const [filters, setFilters] = useState<WorkerFilters>({ ...DEFAULT_WORKER_FILTERS, ...initial })

  function update(patch: Partial<WorkerFilters>) {
    // any real filter change resets pagination back to page 1
    const resetsPage = Object.keys(patch).some((k) => k !== 'page')
    setFilters((prev) => ({ ...prev, ...patch, page: resetsPage ? 1 : (patch.page ?? prev.page) }))
  }

  function reset() {
    setFilters(DEFAULT_WORKER_FILTERS)
  }

  return { filters, update, reset }
}

export function useWorkerDetail(workerId: string) {
  const { data, error, isLoading, mutate } = useSWR<WorkerDetail>(
    workerId ? `/api/admin/workers/${workerId}` : null,
    fetcher,
    { refreshInterval: 30_000 } // keeps the presence indicator fresh
  )
  return { worker: data, isLoading, error, mutate }
}

export function useWorkerPerformance(workerId: string) {
  const { data, error, isLoading, mutate } = useSWR<WorkerPerformance>(
    workerId ? `/api/admin/workers/${workerId}/performance` : null,
    fetcher
  )
  return { stats: data, isLoading, error, mutate }
}

export function useWorkerEarnings(workerId: string) {
  const { data, error, isLoading, mutate } = useSWR<WorkerEarnings>(
    workerId ? `/api/admin/workers/${workerId}/earnings` : null,
    fetcher
  )
  return { earnings: data, isLoading, error, mutate }
}

export function useWorkerJobs(workerId: string) {
  const { data, error, isLoading, mutate } = useSWR<AvailabilityAwareList<WorkerJobRow>>(
    workerId ? `/api/admin/workers/${workerId}/jobs` : null,
    fetcher
  )
  return { jobs: data?.data ?? [], available: data?.available ?? true, isLoading, error, mutate }
}

export function useWorkerReviews(workerId: string) {
  const { data, error, isLoading, mutate } = useSWR<AvailabilityAwareList<WorkerReviewRow>>(
    workerId ? `/api/admin/workers/${workerId}/reviews` : null,
    fetcher
  )
  return { reviews: data?.data ?? [], available: data?.available ?? true, isLoading, error, mutate }
}

export function useWorkerAvailability(workerId: string) {
  const { data, error, isLoading, mutate } = useSWR<WorkerAvailabilityData>(
    workerId ? `/api/admin/workers/${workerId}/availability` : null,
    fetcher
  )
  return { availability: data, isLoading, error, mutate }
}

export function useWorkerCatalog(workerId: string) {
  const { data, error, isLoading, mutate } = useSWR<WorkerCatalog>(
    workerId ? `/api/admin/workers/${workerId}/catalog` : null,
    fetcher
  )
  return { catalog: data, isLoading, error, mutate }
}

/** `enabled = false` skips the request entirely (admin lacks workers:verify). */
export function useWorkerDocuments(workerId: string, enabled: boolean) {
  const { data, error, isLoading, mutate } = useSWR<WorkerDocumentRow[]>(
    workerId && enabled ? `/api/admin/workers/${workerId}/documents` : null,
    fetcher
  )
  return { documents: data ?? [], isLoading, error, mutate }
}

// --- Mutations ---

async function send(method: 'POST' | 'PATCH', url: string, body?: object) {
  const res = await fetch(url, {
    method,
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify(body ?? {}),
  })
  const json = await res.json().catch(() => ({}))
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(json.error || 'Action failed')
  return json
}

export const workerActions = {
  warn: (workerId: string, reason: string) =>
    send('POST', `/api/admin/workers/${workerId}/actions/warn`, { reason }),
  suspend: (
    workerId: string,
    reason: string,
    duration: '7d' | '30d' | 'custom',
    until?: string
  ) => send('POST', `/api/admin/workers/${workerId}/actions/suspend`, { reason, duration, until }),
  unsuspend: (workerId: string) =>
    send('POST', `/api/admin/workers/${workerId}/actions/unsuspend`),
  ban: (workerId: string, reason: string) =>
    send('POST', `/api/admin/workers/${workerId}/actions/ban`, { reason }),
  editContact: (workerId: string, phone?: string, email?: string) =>
    send('PATCH', `/api/admin/workers/${workerId}/actions/edit-contact`, { phone, email }),
  setFeatured: (workerId: string, featured: boolean) =>
    send('POST', `/api/admin/workers/${workerId}/actions/feature`, { featured }),
  setBadge: (workerId: string, badge: WorkerBadgeKey, action: 'add' | 'remove') =>
    send('POST', `/api/admin/workers/${workerId}/actions/badge`, { badge, action }),
  forceOffline: (workerId: string, reason: string) =>
    send('POST', `/api/admin/workers/${workerId}/actions/force-offline`, { reason }),
  requestReverification: (workerId: string, reason: string) =>
    send('POST', `/api/admin/workers/${workerId}/actions/request-reverification`, { reason }),
}

FILE: hooks\useUsers.ts

// hooks/useUsers.ts
import useSWR from 'swr'
import { useState } from 'react'
import { fetcher } from '@/lib/swr-fetcher'
import type {
  UserListResponse, UserFilters, UserDetail, UserAddress,
  BlockedWorker, AvailabilityAwareList, UserBookingRow, UserReviewRow, UserReportRow,
} from '@/types/user'

export interface UserKpis {
  totalUsers: number
  signupsToday: number
  signupsTodayChangePct: number | null
  activeUsers: number
  suspendedOrBanned: number
  signupsSparkline7d: number[]
}

export const DEFAULT_USER_FILTERS: UserFilters = {
  search: '',
  city: 'all',
  status: 'all',
  bookingsBucket: 'all',
  hasDispute: 'all',
  dateFrom: null,
  dateTo: null,
  sortBy: 'created_at',
  sortDir: 'desc',
  page: 1,
  pageSize: 20,
}

function buildQuery(filters: UserFilters) {
  const qs = new URLSearchParams()
  if (filters.search) qs.set('search', filters.search)
  if (filters.city !== 'all') qs.set('city', filters.city)
  if (filters.status !== 'all') qs.set('status', filters.status)
  if (filters.bookingsBucket !== 'all') qs.set('bookingsBucket', filters.bookingsBucket)
  if (filters.hasDispute !== 'all') qs.set('hasDispute', filters.hasDispute)
  if (filters.dateFrom) qs.set('dateFrom', filters.dateFrom)
  if (filters.dateTo) qs.set('dateTo', filters.dateTo)
  qs.set('sortBy', filters.sortBy)
  qs.set('sortDir', filters.sortDir)
  qs.set('page', String(filters.page))
  qs.set('pageSize', String(filters.pageSize))
  return qs.toString()
}

export function useUserKpis() {
  const { data, error, isLoading } = useSWR<UserKpis>('/api/admin/users/kpis', fetcher)
  return { kpis: data, isLoading, error }
}

export function useUsersList(filters: UserFilters) {
  const { data, error, isLoading, mutate } = useSWR<UserListResponse>(
    `/api/admin/users?${buildQuery(filters)}`,
    fetcher,
    { keepPreviousData: true }
  )
  return { users: data, isLoading, error, mutate }
}

export function useUserFilters(initial: Partial<UserFilters> = {}) {
  const [filters, setFilters] = useState<UserFilters>({ ...DEFAULT_USER_FILTERS, ...initial })

  function update(patch: Partial<UserFilters>) {
    // any real filter change resets pagination back to page 1
    const resetsPage = Object.keys(patch).some((k) => k !== 'page')
    setFilters((prev) => ({ ...prev, ...patch, page: resetsPage ? 1 : (patch.page ?? prev.page) }))
  }

  function reset() {
    setFilters(DEFAULT_USER_FILTERS)
  }

  return { filters, update, reset }
}

export function useUserDetail(userId: string) {
  const { data, error, isLoading, mutate } = useSWR<UserDetail>(
    userId ? `/api/admin/users/${userId}` : null,
    fetcher
  )
  return { user: data, isLoading, error, mutate }
}

// --- Mutations ---

async function postAction(url: string, body?: object) {
  const res = await fetch(url, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify(body ?? {}),
  })
  const json = await res.json()
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(json.error || 'Action failed')
  return json
}

export const userActions = {
  warn: (userId: string, reason: string) =>
    postAction(`/api/admin/users/${userId}/actions/warn`, { reason }),
  suspend: (userId: string, reason: string, duration: '7d' | '30d' | 'custom', until?: string) =>
    postAction(`/api/admin/users/${userId}/actions/suspend`, { reason, duration, until }),
  unsuspend: (userId: string) =>
    postAction(`/api/admin/users/${userId}/actions/unsuspend`),
  ban: (userId: string, reason: string) =>
    postAction(`/api/admin/users/${userId}/actions/ban`, { reason }),
  editContact: async (userId: string, phone?: string, email?: string) => {
    const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/users/${userId}/actions/edit-contact`, {
      method: 'PATCH',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ phone, email }),
    })
    const json = await res.json()
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(json.error || 'Update failed')
    return json
  },
  impersonate: (userId: string) =>
    postAction(`/api/admin/users/${userId}/actions/impersonate`),
}

// lib/workers-api.ts
// Shared helpers for the /api/admin/workers/* routes.
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server'

const UUID_RE = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i
const DATE_RE = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/
const CATEGORY_RE = /^[a-z0-9-]{1,50}$/i

export const isUuid = (value: string) => UUID_RE.test(value)

export const workerNotFound = () =>
  NextResponse.json({ error: 'Worker not found' }, { status: 404 })

// SQL fragments, defined once so list + export can never disagree.
export const STATUS_SQL =
  'fn_worker_status(w.is_active, w.deleted_at, w.banned_at, w.suspended_until)'
export const PRESENCE_SQL =
  'fn_worker_presence(w.is_active, w.manually_paused, w.is_on_job, w.last_seen_at)'
export const CATEGORY_NAMES_SQL = `(
  SELECT string_agg(c.name, ', ' ORDER BY c.sort_order, c.name)
  FROM worker_skills ws
  JOIN categories c ON c.id = ws.category_id
  WHERE ws.worker_id = w.id
)`

export const SORT_COLUMNS: Record<string, string> = {
  created_at: 'w.created_at',
  name: 'w.name',
  total_bookings: 'w.total_bookings',
  avg_rating: 'w.avg_rating',
}

const ACCOUNT_STATUSES = ['active', 'inactive', 'suspended', 'banned', 'deleted']
const PRESENCES = ['online', 'away', 'offline', 'paused', 'on_job']
const VERIFICATIONS = ['unverified', 'pending_review', 'verified', 'rejected', 'suspended']

// Only workers who have reviews can be "below 3.0": a brand-new worker has
// avg_rating = 0 and must not show up as a problem worker.
const RATING_BUCKETS: Record<string, string> = {
  lt3: 'w.total_reviews > 0 AND w.avg_rating < 3',
  '3-4': 'w.total_reviews > 0 AND w.avg_rating >= 3 AND w.avg_rating < 4',
  '4+': 'w.total_reviews > 0 AND w.avg_rating >= 4',
  unrated: 'COALESCE(w.total_reviews, 0) = 0',
}

export function buildWorkerWhere(sp: URLSearchParams): { whereSql: string; params: unknown[] } {
  const where: string[] = []
  const params: unknown[] = []
  const push = (value: unknown) => {
    params.push(value)
    return `$${params.length}`
  }

  const search = (sp.get('search') || '').trim()
  const city = sp.get('city') || 'all'
  const categoryId = sp.get('categoryId') || 'all'
  const verification = sp.get('verification') || 'all'
  const status = sp.get('status') || 'all'
  const presence = sp.get('presence') || 'all'
  const ratingBucket = sp.get('ratingBucket') || 'all'
  const dateFrom = sp.get('dateFrom')
  const dateTo = sp.get('dateTo')

  if (search) {
    const p = push(`%${search}%`)
    where.push(`(w.name ILIKE ${p} OR w.phone ILIKE ${p} OR w.email ILIKE ${p})`)
  }

  if (city !== 'all') {
    where.push(`w.city = ${push(city)}`)
  }

  if (categoryId !== 'all' && CATEGORY_RE.test(categoryId)) {
    where.push(
      `EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM worker_skills ws WHERE ws.worker_id = w.id AND ws.category_id = ${push(categoryId)})`
    )
  }

  if (VERIFICATIONS.includes(verification)) {
    where.push(`w.verification_status::text = ${push(verification.toUpperCase())}`)
  }

  // Deleted workers are hidden unless you explicitly filter for them,
  // so the list total matches the "Total Workers" KPI.
  if (ACCOUNT_STATUSES.includes(status)) {
    where.push(`${STATUS_SQL} = ${push(status)}`)
  } else {
    where.push('w.deleted_at IS NULL')
  }

  if (PRESENCES.includes(presence)) {
    where.push(`${PRESENCE_SQL} = ${push(presence)}`)
  }

  if (RATING_BUCKETS[ratingBucket]) {
    where.push(`(${RATING_BUCKETS[ratingBucket]})`)
  }

  if (dateFrom && DATE_RE.test(dateFrom)) {
    where.push(`w.created_at >= ${push(dateFrom)}::date`)
  }
  if (dateTo && DATE_RE.test(dateTo)) {
    // inclusive of the whole "to" day
    where.push(`w.created_at < (${push(dateTo)}::date + 1)`)
  }

  return { whereSql: where.length ? `WHERE ${where.join(' AND ')}` : '', params }
}

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: app\api\admin\workers\route.ts
------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// app/api/admin/workers/route.ts
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'
import { db } from '@/lib/db'
import { requirePermission } from '@/lib/rbac-guard'
import {
  buildWorkerWhere,
  CATEGORY_NAMES_SQL,
  PRESENCE_SQL,
  SORT_COLUMNS,
  STATUS_SQL,
} from '@/lib/workers-api'

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  const guard = await requirePermission('workers:view')
  if (!guard.ok) return guard.response

  const sp = req.nextUrl.searchParams
  const { whereSql, params } = buildWorkerWhere(sp)

  const sortBy = SORT_COLUMNS[sp.get('sortBy') || 'created_at'] || 'w.created_at'
  const sortDir = sp.get('sortDir') === 'asc' ? 'ASC' : 'DESC'
  const page = Math.max(1, parseInt(sp.get('page') || '1', 10) || 1)
  const pageSize = Math.min(100, Math.max(1, parseInt(sp.get('pageSize') || '20', 10) || 20))
  const offset = (page - 1) * pageSize

  const listSql = `
    SELECT
      w.id, w.name, w.phone, w.email, w.avatar_url, w.city,
      w.avg_rating, w.total_reviews, w.total_bookings,
      w.is_active, w.is_featured, w.created_at,
      lower(w.verification_status::text) AS verification_status,
      ${STATUS_SQL}   AS status,
      ${PRESENCE_SQL} AS presence,
      ${CATEGORY_NAMES_SQL} AS categories
    FROM workers w
    ${whereSql}
    ORDER BY ${sortBy} ${sortDir}, w.id
    LIMIT $${params.length + 1} OFFSET $${params.length + 2}
  `
  const countSql = `SELECT count(*)::int AS total FROM workers w ${whereSql}`

  try {
    const [listRes, countRes] = await Promise.all([
      db.query(listSql, [...params, pageSize, offset]),
      db.query(countSql, params),
    ])

    const data = listRes.rows.map((r) => ({
      id: r.id,
      name: r.name,
      phone: r.phone,
      email: r.email,
      avatarUrl: r.avatar_url,
      city: r.city,
      categories: r.categories,
      avgRating: Number(r.avg_rating ?? 0),
      totalReviews: r.total_reviews ?? 0,
      totalBookings: r.total_bookings ?? 0,
      isActive: r.is_active,
      isFeatured: r.is_featured,
      verificationStatus: r.verification_status,
      status: r.status,
      presence: r.presence,
      createdAt: r.created_at,
    }))

    const total = countRes.rows[0]?.total ?? 0

    return NextResponse.json({
      data,
      page,
      pageSize,
      total,
      totalPages: Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / pageSize)),
    })
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('[GET /api/admin/workers]', err)
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Failed to load workers' }, { status: 500 })
  }
}

// app/api/admin/workers/filter-options/route.ts
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server'

export async function GET() {
  const guard = await requirePermission('workers:view')
  if (!guard.ok) return guard.response

  try {
    const { rows } = await db.query(
      `SELECT id, name FROM categories WHERE is_active = true ORDER BY sort_order, name`
    )
    return NextResponse.json({
      categories: rows.map((r) => ({ id: r.id, name: r.name })),
    })

  }

// app/api/admin/workers/kpis/route.ts
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server'
import { db } from '@/lib/db'
import { requirePermission } from '@/lib/rbac-guard'

export async function GET() {
  const guard = await requirePermission('workers:view')
  if (!guard.ok) return guard.response

  try {
    const result = await db.query('SELECT * FROM get_worker_kpis()')
    const row = result.rows[0]

    const signupsToday = Number(row.signups_today)
    const signupsYesterday = Number(row.signups_yesterday)
    const signupsTodayChangePct =
      signupsYesterday === 0
        ? signupsToday === 0
          ? 0
          : 100
        : ((signupsToday - signupsYesterday) / signupsYesterday) * 100

    return NextResponse.json({
      totalWorkers: Number(row.total_workers),
      signupsToday,
      signupsTodayChangePct,
      onlineNow: Number(row.online_now),
      suspendedOrBanned: Number(row.suspended_or_banned),
      signupsSparkline7d: (row.signups_sparkline_7d ?? []).map((n: number | string) => Number(n)),
    })
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('[GET /api/admin/workers/kpis]', err)
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Failed to load worker KPIs' }, { status: 500 })
  }
}

// app/api/admin/users/route.ts
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'
import { db } from '@/lib/db'
import { requirePermission } from '@/lib/rbac-guard'

const BOOKING_BUCKETS: Record<string, string> = {
  '0': 'u.total_bookings = 0',
  '1-5': 'u.total_bookings BETWEEN 1 AND 5',
  '5-20': 'u.total_bookings BETWEEN 6 AND 20',
  '20+': 'u.total_bookings > 20',
}

const SORT_COLUMNS: Record<string, string> = {
  created_at: 'u.created_at',
  name: 'u.name',
  total_bookings: 'u.total_bookings',
}

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  const guard = await requirePermission('users:view')
  if (!guard.ok) return guard.response

  const sp = req.nextUrl.searchParams
  const search = (sp.get('search') || '').trim()
  const city = sp.get('city') || 'all'
  const status = sp.get('status') || 'all'
  const bookingsBucket = sp.get('bookingsBucket') || 'all'
  const hasDispute = sp.get('hasDispute') || 'all'
  const dateFrom = sp.get('dateFrom')
  const dateTo = sp.get('dateTo')
  const sortBy = SORT_COLUMNS[sp.get('sortBy') || 'created_at'] || 'u.created_at'
  const sortDir = sp.get('sortDir') === 'asc' ? 'ASC' : 'DESC'
  const page = Math.max(1, parseInt(sp.get('page') || '1', 10))
  const pageSize = Math.min(100, Math.max(1, parseInt(sp.get('pageSize') || '20', 10)))
  const offset = (page - 1) * pageSize

  const where: string[] = ['u.deleted_at IS NULL OR $__include_deleted']
  // deleted_at handling folded into status filter below instead of a blanket exclude
  where.length = 0

  const params: any[] = []
  let i = 1

  if (search) {
    params.push(`%${search}%`)
    where.push(`(u.name ILIKE $${i} OR u.phone ILIKE $${i} OR u.email ILIKE $${i})`)
    i++
  }

  if (city !== 'all') {
    params.push(city)
    where.push(`EXISTS (
      SELECT 1 FROM user_addresses a
      WHERE a.user_id = u.id AND a.is_default = true AND a.city = $${i}
    )`)
    i++
  }

  if (status !== 'all') {
    params.push(status)
    where.push(`fn_user_status(u.is_active, u.deleted_at, u.banned_at, u.suspended_until) = $${i}`)
    i++
  }

  if (bookingsBucket !== 'all' && BOOKING_BUCKETS[bookingsBucket]) {
    where.push(BOOKING_BUCKETS[bookingsBucket])
  }

  if (dateFrom) {
    params.push(dateFrom)
    where.push(`u.created_at >= $${i}`)
    i++
  }
  if (dateTo) {
    params.push(dateTo)
    where.push(`u.created_at <= $${i}`)
    i++
  }

  // has_dispute: disputes table doesn't exist yet in the schema I was given.
  // Kept as a no-op filter (accepted, ignored) so the UI control works
  // end-to-end without erroring â€” wire the EXISTS(...) once disputes ships.
  void hasDispute

  const whereSql = where.length ? `WHERE ${where.join(' AND ')}` : ''

  const listSql = `
    SELECT
      u.id, u.name, u.phone, u.email, u.avatar_url,
      u.total_bookings, u.is_active, u.created_at,
      fn_user_status(u.is_active, u.deleted_at, u.banned_at, u.suspended_until) AS status,
      (SELECT a.city FROM user_addresses a WHERE a.user_id = u.id AND a.is_default = true LIMIT 1) AS city
    FROM users u
    ${whereSql}
    ORDER BY ${sortBy} ${sortDir}
    LIMIT $${i} OFFSET $${i + 1}
  `
  const countSql = `SELECT count(*)::int AS total FROM users u ${whereSql}`

  const listParams = [...params, pageSize, offset]

  try {
    const [listRes, countRes] = await Promise.all([
      db.query(listSql, listParams),
      db.query(countSql, params),
    ])

    const data = listRes.rows.map((r) => ({
      id: r.id,
      name: r.name,
      phone: r.phone,
      email: r.email,
      avatarUrl: r.avatar_url,
      city: r.city,
      totalBookings: r.total_bookings,
      isActive: r.is_active,
      status: r.status,
      hasDispute: false,
      createdAt: r.created_at,
    }))

    const total = countRes.rows[0]?.total ?? 0

    return NextResponse.json({
      data,
      page,
      pageSize,
      total,
      totalPages: Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / pageSize)),
    })
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('[GET /api/admin/users]', err)
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Failed to load users' }, { status: 500 })
  }
}

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: app\api\admin\workers\[workerId]\actions\warn\route.ts
------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// app/api/admin/workers/[workerId]/actions/warn/route.ts
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'
import { db } from '@/lib/db'
import { requirePermission } from '@/lib/rbac-guard'
import { logAdminAction, getClientIp } from '@/lib/audit'
import { isUuid, workerNotFound } from '@/lib/workers-api'

export async function POST(
  req: NextRequest,
  { params }: { params: Promise<{ workerId: string }> }
) {
  const guard = await requirePermission('workers:warn')
  if (!guard.ok) return guard.response

  const { workerId } = await params
  if (!isUuid(workerId)) return workerNotFound()

  const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}))
  const reason: string = body.reason?.trim()
  if (!reason) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'A reason is required' }, { status: 400 })
  }

  const { rows: before } = await db.query(
    `SELECT warning_count FROM workers WHERE id = $1 AND deleted_at IS NULL`,
    [workerId]
  )
  if (before.length === 0) return workerNotFound()

  const { rows } = await db.query(
    `UPDATE workers
     SET warning_count = warning_count + 1,
         last_warning_at = now(),
         last_warning_reason = $2,
         updated_at = now()
     WHERE id = $1
     RETURNING warning_count`,
    [workerId, reason]
  )

  await logAdminAction({
    adminId: guard.admin.adminId,
    adminEmail: guard.admin.adminEmail,
    action: 'worker.warned',
    resourceType: 'worker',
    resourceId: workerId,
    oldValue: { warningCount: before[0].warning_count },
    newValue: { warningCount: rows[0].warning_count, reason },
    notes: reason,
    ip: getClientIp(req),
    userAgent: req.headers.get('user-agent') ?? undefined,
  })

  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, warningCount: rows[0].warning_count })
}

// app/api/admin/users/[userId]/actions/warn/route.ts
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'
import { db } from '@/lib/db'
import { requirePermission } from '@/lib/rbac-guard'
import { logAdminAction, getClientIp } from '@/lib/audit'

export async function POST(
  req: NextRequest,
  { params }: { params: Promise<{ userId: string }> }
) {
  const guard = await requirePermission('users:warn')
  if (!guard.ok) return guard.response

  const { userId } = await params

  const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}))
  const reason: string = body.reason?.trim()
  if (!reason) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'A reason is required' }, { status: 400 })
  }

  const { rows: before } = await db.query(
    `SELECT warning_count FROM users WHERE id = $1`,
    [userId]
  )
  if (before.length === 0) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'User not found' }, { status: 404 })
  }

  const { rows } = await db.query(
    `UPDATE users
     SET warning_count = warning_count + 1,
         last_warning_at = now(),
         last_warning_reason = $2,
         updated_at = now()
     WHERE id = $1
     RETURNING warning_count, last_warning_at`,
    [userId, reason]
  )

  await logAdminAction({
    adminId: guard.admin.adminId,
    adminEmail: guard.admin.adminEmail,
    action: 'user.warned',
    resourceType: 'user',
    resourceId: userId,
    oldValue: { warningCount: before[0].warning_count },
    newValue: { warningCount: rows[0].warning_count, reason },
    notes: reason,
    ip: getClientIp(req),
    userAgent: req.headers.get('user-agent') ?? undefined,
  })

  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, warningCount: rows[0].warning_count })
}

-- 004_users_moderation.sql
-- Adds admin-moderation columns to users, plus search index.
-- Status is derived at query time, not stored as an enum column, so it can
-- never drift out of sync with the underlying timestamps.

ALTER TABLE public.users
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS suspended_until   timestamptz NULL,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS suspended_reason  text NULL,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS suspended_by      uuid NULL REFERENCES admin_users(id),
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS banned_at         timestamptz NULL,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS banned_reason     text NULL,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS banned_by         uuid NULL REFERENCES admin_users(id),
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS warning_count     integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS last_warning_at   timestamptz NULL,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS last_warning_reason text NULL;

-- Trigram search across name / phone / email (pg_trgm already enabled in 003)
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_users_name_trgm  ON public.users USING gin (name gin_trgm_ops);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_users_phone_trgm ON public.users USING gin (phone gin_trgm_ops);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_users_email_trgm ON public.users USING gin (email gin_trgm_ops);

-- Helper: derives a single status string from the timestamp columns.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.fn_user_status(
  p_is_active   boolean,
  p_deleted_at  timestamptz,
  p_banned_at   timestamptz,
  p_suspended_until timestamptz
) RETURNS text AS $$
BEGIN
  IF p_deleted_at IS NOT NULL THEN
    RETURN 'deleted';
  ELSIF p_banned_at IS NOT NULL THEN
    RETURN 'banned';
  ELSIF p_suspended_until IS NOT NULL AND p_suspended_until > now() THEN
    RETURN 'suspended';
  ELSIF p_is_active THEN
    RETURN 'active';
  ELSE
    RETURN 'inactive';
  END IF;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql IMMUTABLE;

-- Single-user overview: profile + address count + blocked-worker count +
-- default city, in one round trip for the detail page header.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.get_user_overview(p_user_id uuid)
RETURNS TABLE (
  id uuid, phone varchar, email varchar, name varchar, avatar_url text,
  preferred_language varchar, preferred_currency varchar,
  total_bookings integer, is_active boolean, role text,
  created_at timestamptz, updated_at timestamptz, deleted_at timestamptz,
  push_enabled boolean, email_enabled boolean,
  suspended_until timestamptz, suspended_reason text, banned_at timestamptz,
  banned_reason text, warning_count integer, last_warning_at timestamptz,
  last_warning_reason text, status text,
  default_city varchar, address_count bigint, blocked_worker_count bigint
) AS $$
  SELECT
    u.id, u.phone, u.email, u.name, u.avatar_url,
    u.preferred_language, u.preferred_currency,
    u.total_bookings, u.is_active, u.role,
    u.created_at, u.updated_at, u.deleted_at,
    u.push_enabled, u.email_enabled,
    u.suspended_until, u.suspended_reason, u.banned_at,
    u.banned_reason, u.warning_count, u.last_warning_at,
    u.last_warning_reason,
    fn_user_status(u.is_active, u.deleted_at, u.banned_at, u.suspended_until) AS status,
    (SELECT a.city FROM user_addresses a WHERE a.user_id = u.id AND a.is_default = true LIMIT 1) AS default_city,
    (SELECT count(*) FROM user_addresses a WHERE a.user_id = u.id) AS address_count,
    (SELECT count(*) FROM user_blocks b WHERE b.user_id = u.id) AS blocked_worker_count
  FROM users u
  WHERE u.id = p_user_id;
$$ LANGUAGE sql STABLE;

-- 006_workers_moderation.sql
-- Admin-moderation columns for workers, derived status/presence helpers,
-- KPI + overview functions, and worker badges.
--
-- NOTE: `workers` is owned by worker-service. Everything here is additive
-- (ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS), so it is safe on the shared DB, but tell the
-- worker-service team (see section 12).

CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS pg_trgm;

ALTER TABLE public.workers
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS suspended_until              timestamptz NULL,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS suspended_reason             text NULL,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS suspended_by                 uuid NULL REFERENCES admin_users(id),
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS banned_at                    timestamptz NULL,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS banned_reason                text NULL,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS banned_by                    uuid NULL REFERENCES admin_users(id),
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS warning_count                integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS last_warning_at              timestamptz NULL,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS last_warning_reason          text NULL,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS is_featured                  boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS featured_at                  timestamptz NULL,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS reverification_requested_at  timestamptz NULL,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS reverification_reason        text NULL;

-- Search + filter indexes
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_workers_name_trgm  ON public.workers USING gin (name  gin_trgm_ops);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_workers_phone_trgm ON public.workers USING gin (phone gin_trgm_ops);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_workers_email_trgm ON public.workers USING gin (email gin_trgm_ops);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_workers_verification_status ON public.workers (verification_status);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_workers_city        ON public.workers (city);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_workers_created_at  ON public.workers (created_at DESC);

-- Admin-awarded badges ("Top Rated", "Background Verified")
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.worker_badges (
  id          uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  worker_id   uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES public.workers(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  badge       varchar(50) NOT NULL CHECK (badge IN ('top_rated', 'background_verified')),
  awarded_by  uuid NULL REFERENCES admin_users(id),
  created_at  timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  UNIQUE (worker_id, badge)
);

-- Account status, derived from timestamps (same idea as fn_user_status).
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.fn_worker_status(
  p_is_active       boolean,
  p_deleted_at      timestamptz,
  p_banned_at       timestamptz,
  p_suspended_until timestamptz
) RETURNS text AS $$
BEGIN
  IF p_deleted_at IS NOT NULL THEN
    RETURN 'deleted';
  ELSIF p_banned_at IS NOT NULL THEN
    RETURN 'banned';
  ELSIF p_suspended_until IS NOT NULL AND p_suspended_until > now() THEN
    RETURN 'suspended';
  ELSIF p_is_active THEN
    RETURN 'active';
  ELSE
    RETURN 'inactive';
  END IF;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE;

-- Live presence, derived from last_seen_at (worker-service doc section 7).
--   inactive account -> offline | manually paused -> paused | on a job -> on_job
--   < 90 s -> online | 90 s - 10 min -> away | > 10 min -> offline
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.fn_worker_presence(
  p_is_active       boolean,
  p_manually_paused boolean,
  p_is_on_job       boolean,
  p_last_seen_at    timestamptz
) RETURNS text AS $$
DECLARE
  secs numeric;
BEGIN
  IF NOT COALESCE(p_is_active, false) THEN
    RETURN 'offline';
  ELSIF COALESCE(p_manually_paused, false) THEN
    RETURN 'paused';
  ELSIF COALESCE(p_is_on_job, false) THEN
    RETURN 'on_job';
  END IF;

  IF p_last_seen_at IS NULL THEN
    RETURN 'offline';
  END IF;

  secs := EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (now() - p_last_seen_at));
  IF secs < 90 THEN
    RETURN 'online';
  ELSIF secs < 600 THEN
    RETURN 'away';
  ELSE
    RETURN 'offline';
  END IF;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE;

-- KPI cards for the list page. "Today" uses Asia/Karachi.
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.get_worker_kpis();
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.get_worker_kpis()
RETURNS TABLE (
  total_workers        bigint,
  signups_today        bigint,
  signups_yesterday    bigint,
  online_now           bigint,
  suspended_or_banned  bigint,
  signups_sparkline_7d bigint[]
) AS $$
  WITH b AS (
    SELECT (date_trunc('day', now() AT TIME ZONE 'Asia/Karachi') AT TIME ZONE 'Asia/Karachi') AS today_start
  )
  SELECT
    (SELECT count(*) FROM workers w WHERE w.deleted_at IS NULL),
    (SELECT count(*) FROM workers w
       WHERE w.deleted_at IS NULL AND w.created_at >= b.today_start),
    (SELECT count(*) FROM workers w
       WHERE w.deleted_at IS NULL
         AND w.created_at >= b.today_start - interval '1 day'
         AND w.created_at <  b.today_start),
    (SELECT count(*) FROM workers w
       WHERE w.deleted_at IS NULL
         AND fn_worker_presence(w.is_active, w.manually_paused, w.is_on_job, w.last_seen_at) = 'online'),
    (SELECT count(*) FROM workers w
       WHERE w.deleted_at IS NULL
         AND fn_worker_status(w.is_active, w.deleted_at, w.banned_at, w.suspended_until) IN ('suspended', 'banned')),
    (SELECT array_agg(s.c ORDER BY s.d)
       FROM (
         SELECT g.d,
                (SELECT count(*) FROM workers w
                   WHERE w.deleted_at IS NULL
                     AND w.created_at >= b.today_start - (6 - g.d) * interval '1 day'
                     AND w.created_at <  b.today_start - (5 - g.d) * interval '1 day') AS c
         FROM generate_series(0, 6) AS g(d)
       ) s)
  FROM b;
$$ LANGUAGE sql STABLE;

-- Single-worker overview: profile + counts + skills + badges in one round trip.
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.get_worker_overview(uuid);
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.get_worker_overview(p_worker_id uuid)
RETURNS TABLE (
  id uuid, phone varchar, email varchar, name varchar, bio text, avatar_url text,
  verification_status text, manually_paused boolean, is_on_job boolean, is_active boolean,
  last_seen_at timestamptz, currency varchar, avg_rating numeric, total_reviews integer,
  total_bookings integer, response_time_mins integer, years_experience integer,
  city varchar, country varchar, preferred_language varchar,
  created_at timestamptz, updated_at timestamptz, deleted_at timestamptz,
  suspended_until timestamptz, suspended_reason text, banned_at timestamptz, banned_reason text,
  warning_count integer, last_warning_at timestamptz, last_warning_reason text,
  is_featured boolean, featured_at timestamptz,
  reverification_requested_at timestamptz, reverification_reason text,
  status text, presence text,
  skill_count bigint, offering_count bigint, document_count bigint, pending_document_count bigint,
  skills text[], badges text[]
) AS $$
  SELECT
    w.id, w.phone, w.email, w.name, w.bio, w.avatar_url,
    lower(w.verification_status::text), w.manually_paused, w.is_on_job, w.is_active,
    w.last_seen_at, w.currency, w.avg_rating, w.total_reviews,
    w.total_bookings, w.response_time_mins, w.years_experience,
    w.city, w.country, w.preferred_language,
    w.created_at, w.updated_at, w.deleted_at,
    w.suspended_until, w.suspended_reason, w.banned_at, w.banned_reason,
    w.warning_count, w.last_warning_at, w.last_warning_reason,
    w.is_featured, w.featured_at,
    w.reverification_requested_at, w.reverification_reason,
    fn_worker_status(w.is_active, w.deleted_at, w.banned_at, w.suspended_until),
    fn_worker_presence(w.is_active, w.manually_paused, w.is_on_job, w.last_seen_at),
    (SELECT count(*) FROM worker_skills s WHERE s.worker_id = w.id),
    (SELECT count(*) FROM worker_service_offerings o WHERE o.worker_id = w.id AND o.is_active = true),
    (SELECT count(*) FROM worker_documents d WHERE d.worker_id = w.id),
    (SELECT count(*) FROM worker_documents d WHERE d.worker_id = w.id AND d.status = 'PENDING'),
    ARRAY(
      SELECT c.name::text
      FROM worker_skills s JOIN categories c ON c.id = s.category_id
      WHERE s.worker_id = w.id
      ORDER BY c.sort_order, c.name
    ),
    ARRAY(
      SELECT b.badge::text FROM worker_badges b
      WHERE b.worker_id = w.id ORDER BY b.created_at
    )
  FROM workers w
  WHERE w.id = p_worker_id;
$$ LANGUAGE sql STABLE;

SECTION 7. CATEGORIES (audience dropdowns + form dialog)
