------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: app\api\admin\categories\route.ts
------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'
import { db } from '@/lib/db'
import { requirePermission } from '@/lib/rbac-guard'
import { logAdminAction, getClientIp } from '@/lib/audit'

const SORT_COLUMNS: Record<string, string> = {
  sort_order: 'c.sort_order',
  name: 'c.name',
  active_workers: 'active_worker_count',
}

const ID_SLUG_RE = /^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]{1,48}[a-z0-9]$/

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  const guard = await requirePermission('categories:view')
  if (!guard.ok) return guard.response

  const sp = req.nextUrl.searchParams
  const search = (sp.get('search') || '').trim()
  const status = sp.get('status') || 'all'
  const sortBy = SORT_COLUMNS[sp.get('sortBy') || 'sort_order'] || 'c.sort_order'
  const sortDir = sp.get('sortDir') === 'desc' ? 'DESC' : 'ASC'
  const page = Math.max(1, parseInt(sp.get('page') || '1', 10))
  const pageSize = Math.min(100, Math.max(1, parseInt(sp.get('pageSize') || '20', 10)))
  const offset = (page - 1) * pageSize

  const where: string[] = []
  const params: any[] = []
  let i = 1

  if (search) {
    params.push(`%${search}%`)
    where.push(`(c.name ILIKE $${i} OR c.name_ur ILIKE $${i} OR c.id ILIKE $${i})`)
    i++
  }
  if (status !== 'all') {
    params.push(status === 'active')
    where.push(`c.is_active = $${i}`)
    i++
  }

  const whereSql = where.length ? `WHERE ${where.join(' AND ')}` : ''

  // active_worker_count / service_count computed per category via correlated subqueries
  const listSql = `
    SELECT
      c.id, c.name, c.name_ur, c.icon_url, c.parent_id, c.is_active, c.sort_order, c.created_at,
      (SELECT count(*)::int FROM services s WHERE s.category_id = c.id) AS service_count,
      (SELECT count(DISTINCT ws.worker_id)::int
       FROM worker_skills ws
       JOIN workers w ON w.id = ws.worker_id
       WHERE ws.category_id = c.id AND w.verification_status = 'VERIFIED' AND w.is_active = true
      ) AS active_worker_count
    FROM categories c
    ${whereSql}
    ORDER BY ${sortBy} ${sortDir}
    LIMIT $${i} OFFSET $${i + 1}
  `
  const countSql = `SELECT count(*)::int AS total FROM categories c ${whereSql}`
  const listParams = [...params, pageSize, offset]

  try {
    const [listRes, countRes] = await Promise.all([
      db.query(listSql, listParams),
      db.query(countSql, params),
    ])

    const data = listRes.rows.map((r) => ({
      id: r.id,
      name: r.name,
      nameUr: r.name_ur,
      iconUrl: r.icon_url,
      parentId: r.parent_id,
      isActive: r.is_active,
      sortOrder: r.sort_order,
      serviceCount: r.service_count,
      activeWorkerCount: r.active_worker_count,
      createdAt: r.created_at,
    }))
    const total = countRes.rows[0]?.total ?? 0

    return NextResponse.json({
      data, page, pageSize, total,
      totalPages: Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / pageSize)),
    })
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('[GET /api/admin/categories]', err)
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Failed to load categories' }, { status: 500 })
  }
}

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  const guard = await requirePermission('categories:create')
  if (!guard.ok) return guard.response

  const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}))
  const id: string = (body.id || '').trim().toLowerCase()
  const name: string = (body.name || '').trim()

  if (!ID_SLUG_RE.test(id)) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: 'Category ID must be 3â€“50 lowercase letters, numbers, or hyphens (e.g. "ac-repair")' },
      { status: 400 }
    )
  }
  if (!name) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Name is required' }, { status: 400 })
  }

  try {
    const { rows } = await db.query(
      `INSERT INTO categories (id, name, name_ur, icon_url, parent_id, sort_order)
       VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6)
       RETURNING id, name, name_ur, icon_url, parent_id, is_active, sort_order, created_at`,
      [id, name, body.nameUr ?? null, body.iconUrl ?? null, body.parentId ?? null, body.sortOrder ?? 0]
    )

    await logAdminAction({
      adminId: guard.admin.adminId,
      adminEmail: guard.admin.adminEmail,
      action: 'category.created',
      resourceType: 'category',
      resourceId: id,
      newValue: { name, nameUr: body.nameUr ?? null },
      ip: getClientIp(req),
      userAgent: req.headers.get('user-agent') ?? undefined,
    })

    return NextResponse.json({ data: rows[0] }, { status: 201 })
  } catch (err: any) {
    if (err.code === '23505') {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: `Category ID "${id}" already exists` }, { status: 409 })
    }
    console.error('[POST /api/admin/categories]', err)
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Failed to create category' }, { status: 500 })
  }
}

// hooks/useCategories.ts
import useSWR from 'swr'
import { useState } from 'react'
import { fetcher } from '@/lib/swr-fetcher'
import type {
  CategoryListResponse, CategoryFilters, CategoryKpis, CategoryDetail,
  CategoryFormInput, ServiceItem, ServiceFormInput, PricingConfig, PricingConfigInput,
} from '@/types/category'

export const DEFAULT_CATEGORY_FILTERS: CategoryFilters = {
  search: '',
  status: 'all',
  sortBy: 'sort_order',
  sortDir: 'asc',
  page: 1,
  pageSize: 20,
}

function buildQuery(filters: CategoryFilters) {
  const qs = new URLSearchParams()
  if (filters.search) qs.set('search', filters.search)
  if (filters.status !== 'all') qs.set('status', filters.status)
  qs.set('sortBy', filters.sortBy)
  qs.set('sortDir', filters.sortDir)
  qs.set('page', String(filters.page))
  qs.set('pageSize', String(filters.pageSize))
  return qs.toString()
}

export function useCategoryKpis() {
  const { data, error, isLoading } = useSWR<CategoryKpis>('/api/admin/categories/kpis', fetcher)
  return { kpis: data, isLoading, error }
}

export function useCategoriesList(filters: CategoryFilters) {
  const { data, error, isLoading, mutate } = useSWR<CategoryListResponse>(
    `/api/admin/categories?${buildQuery(filters)}`,
    fetcher,
    { keepPreviousData: true }
  )
  return { categories: data, isLoading, error, mutate }
}

export function useCategoryFilters(initial: Partial<CategoryFilters> = {}) {
  const [filters, setFilters] = useState<CategoryFilters>({ ...DEFAULT_CATEGORY_FILTERS, ...initial })

  function update(patch: Partial<CategoryFilters>) {
    const resetsPage = Object.keys(patch).some((k) => k !== 'page')
    setFilters((prev) => ({ ...prev, ...patch, page: resetsPage ? 1 : (patch.page ?? prev.page) }))
  }
  function reset() {
    setFilters(DEFAULT_CATEGORY_FILTERS)
  }
  return { filters, update, reset }
}

export function useCategoryDetail(categoryId: string) {
  const { data, error, isLoading, mutate } = useSWR<CategoryDetail>(
    categoryId ? `/api/admin/categories/${categoryId}` : null,
    fetcher
  )
  return { category: data, isLoading, error, mutate }
}

export function useCategoryServices(categoryId: string) {
  const { data, error, isLoading, mutate } = useSWR<ServiceItem[]>(
    categoryId ? `/api/admin/categories/${categoryId}/services` : null,
    fetcher
  )
  return { services: data ?? [], isLoading, error, mutate }
}

export function usePricingConfig() {
  const { data, error, isLoading, mutate } = useSWR<PricingConfig>('/api/admin/pricing-config', fetcher)
  return { config: data, isLoading, error, mutate }
}

// --- Mutations ---

async function sendAction(url: string, method: 'POST' | 'PATCH', body?: object) {
  const res = await fetch(url, {
    method,
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify(body ?? {}),
  })
  const json = await res.json()
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(json.error || 'Action failed')
  return json
}

export const categoryActions = {
  create: (input: CategoryFormInput) =>
    sendAction('/api/admin/categories', 'POST', input),
  update: (categoryId: string, input: CategoryFormInput) =>
    sendAction(`/api/admin/categories/${categoryId}`, 'PATCH', input),
  toggleActive: (categoryId: string, isActive: boolean) =>
    sendAction(`/api/admin/categories/${categoryId}/actions/toggle-active`, 'POST', { isActive }),
}

export const serviceActions = {
  create: (categoryId: string, input: ServiceFormInput) =>
    sendAction(`/api/admin/categories/${categoryId}/services`, 'POST', input),
  update: (categoryId: string, serviceId: string, input: ServiceFormInput) =>
    sendAction(`/api/admin/categories/${categoryId}/services/${serviceId}`, 'PATCH', input),
  toggleActive: (categoryId: string, serviceId: string, isActive: boolean) =>
    sendAction(
      `/api/admin/categories/${categoryId}/services/${serviceId}/actions/toggle-active`,
      'POST',
      { isActive }
    ),
}

export const pricingConfigActions = {
  update: (input: PricingConfigInput) =>
    sendAction('/api/admin/pricing-config', 'PATCH', input),
}

// types/category.ts

export type PriceType = 'fixed' | 'hourly' | 'quote'

export interface CategoryListItem {
  id: string                 // VARCHAR(50) slug, e.g. "electrician"
  name: string
  nameUr: string | null
  iconUrl: string | null
  parentId: string | null
  isActive: boolean
  sortOrder: number
  serviceCount: number
  activeWorkerCount: number
  createdAt: string
}

export interface CategoryListResponse {
  data: CategoryListItem[]
  page: number
  pageSize: number
  total: number
  totalPages: number
}

export interface CategoryFilters {
  search: string
  status: 'all' | 'active' | 'inactive'
  sortBy: 'sort_order' | 'name' | 'active_workers'
  sortDir: 'asc' | 'desc'
  page: number
  pageSize: number
}

  activeCategories: number
  totalServices: number
  categoriesWithNoActiveWorkers: number
}

  iconUrl?: string | null

  sortOrder?: number
}

'use client'

import { useState } from 'react'
import { toast } from 'sonner'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/Button'
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/Input'
import { categoryActions } from '@/hooks/useCategories'
import type { CategoryListItem } from '@/types/category'

interface Props {
  mode: 'create' | 'edit'
  category?: CategoryListItem
  onClose: () => void
  onSaved: () => void
}

export default function CategoryFormDialog({ mode, category, onClose, onSaved }: Props) {
  const [id, setId] = useState(category?.id ?? '')
  const [name, setName] = useState(category?.name ?? '')
  const [nameUr, setNameUr] = useState(category?.nameUr ?? '')
  const [iconUrl, setIconUrl] = useState(category?.iconUrl ?? '')
  const [sortOrder, setSortOrder] = useState(category?.sortOrder ?? 0)
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)

  async function handleSubmit() {
    setError(null)
    setSubmitting(true)
    try {
      const input = { id: id.trim(), name: name.trim(), nameUr: nameUr.trim() || null, iconUrl: iconUrl.trim() || null, sortOrder }
      if (mode === 'create') {
        await categoryActions.create(input)
        toast.success('Category created')
      } else {
        await categoryActions.update(category!.id, input)
        toast.success('Category updated')
      }
      onSaved()
    } catch (e: any) {
      setError(e.message)
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false)
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/40 px-4">
      <div className="w-full max-w-md rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm">
        <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-gray-900">
          {mode === 'create' ? 'New Category' : `Edit ${category?.name}`}
        </h2>

        <div className="mt-4 flex flex-col gap-3">
          <Input
            label="Category ID"
            placeholder="ac-repair"
            value={id}
            onChange={(e) => setId(e.target.value)}
            disabled={mode === 'edit'}
            helperText={mode === 'create' ? 'Lowercase letters, numbers, hyphens only. Cannot be changed later.' : undefined}
          />
          <Input label="Name" value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} />
          <Input label="Name (Urdu)" value={nameUr} onChange={(e) => setNameUr(e.target.value)} />
          <Input label="Icon URL" value={iconUrl} onChange={(e) => setIconUrl(e.target.value)} />
          <Input
            label="Sort order"
            type="number"
            value={sortOrder}
            onChange={(e) => setSortOrder(Number(e.target.value))}
          />
        </div>

        {error && <p className="mt-3 text-xs text-red-600">{error}</p>}

        <div className="mt-5 flex justify-end gap-2">
          <Button variant="secondary" onClick={onClose} disabled={submitting}>Cancel</Button>
          <Button onClick={handleSubmit} disabled={submitting || !id.trim() || !name.trim()}>
            {submitting ? 'Savingâ€¦' : mode === 'create' ? 'Create' : 'Save'}
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

'use client'

import { useState } from 'react'
import { useMemo } from 'react'
import { useCategoriesList, useCategoryFilters } from '@/hooks/useCategories'
import { usePermission } from '@/hooks/usePermission'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/Button'
import CategoryFilterBar from './CategoryFilterBar'
import CategoryTable from './CategoryTable'
import CategoryFormDialog from './CategoryFormDialog'

export default function CategoriesTableSection() {
  const { filters, update, reset } = useCategoryFilters()
  const { categories, isLoading, error, mutate } = useCategoriesList(filters)
  const canCreate = usePermission('categories:create')
  const [creating, setCreating] = useState(false)

  const totalPages = categories?.totalPages ?? 1

  const pageSummary = useMemo(() => {
    if (!categories) return ''
    const start = (categories.page - 1) * categories.pageSize + 1
    const end = Math.min(categories.page * categories.pageSize, categories.total)
    return `${start}â€“${end} of ${categories.total}`
  }, [categories])

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-gray-500">
          Categories {categories ? `(${categories.total.toLocaleString()})` : ''}
        </h2>
        {canCreate && (
          <Button onClick={() => setCreating(true)}>New Category</Button>
        )}
      </div>

      <CategoryFilterBar filters={filters} onChange={update} onReset={reset} />

      <CategoryTable
        rows={categories?.data ?? []}
        isLoading={isLoading}
        error={error}
        onRetry={() => mutate()}
        onChanged={() => mutate()}
      />

      {categories && categories.total > 0 && (
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-sm text-gray-500">
          <span>{pageSummary}</span>
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <button
              className="h-9 rounded-md border border-gray-300 px-3 disabled:opacity-40"
              disabled={filters.page <= 1}
              onClick={() => update({ page: filters.page - 1 })}
            >
              Previous
            </button>
            <button
              className="h-9 rounded-md border border-gray-300 px-3 disabled:opacity-40"
              disabled={filters.page >= totalPages}
              onClick={() => update({ page: filters.page + 1 })}
            >
              Next
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {creating && (
        <CategoryFormDialog
          mode="create"
          onClose={() => setCreating(false)}
          onSaved={() => {
            setCreating(false)
            mutate()
          }}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

// components/payments/PaymentsSection.tsx
'use client'

import { useState } from 'react'
import { useSearchParams } from 'next/navigation'
import { cn } from '@/lib/cn'
import { LEDGER_STATUSES, PAYMENT_TABS, type PaymentTab } from '@/lib/payment-constants'
import PaymentsOverview from './PaymentsOverview'
import TransactionsTab from './TransactionsTab'
import PayoutsTab from './PayoutsTab'
import RefundsTab from './RefundsTab'
import ReportsTab from './ReportsTab'
import type { PayoutFilters, RefundFilters, TransactionFilters } from '@/types/payment'

const TAB_LABEL: Record<PaymentTab, string> = {
  overview: 'Overview',
  transactions: 'Transactions',
  payouts: 'Worker payouts',
  refunds: 'Refunds',
  reports: 'Reports',
}

const TAB_HINT: Record<PaymentTab, string> = {
  overview: 'Completed-booking revenue, escrow, wallets and anything that needs finance attention.',
  transactions: 'Every online payment plus completed COD bookings. Click a row to open the booking.',
  payouts: 'Money sent to workers (JazzCash, Easypaisa, Raast). Failed payouts can be retried.',
  refunds: 'Every refund, whether automatic (cancellations, disputes) or issued by an admin.',
  reports: 'CSV exports for accounting and FBR preparation.',
}

export default function PaymentsSection() {
  const searchParams = useSearchParams()
  const tabParam = searchParams.get('tab')
  const statusParam = (searchParams.get('status') ?? '').toUpperCase()

  const validTab = (PAYMENT_TABS as readonly string[]).includes(tabParam ?? '')
  const validStatus = (LEDGER_STATUSES as readonly string[]).includes(statusParam)

  // A status deep link (e.g. /payments?status=failed from the dashboard) opens the ledger, filtered.
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<PaymentTab>(
    validTab ? (tabParam as PaymentTab) : validStatus ? 'transactions' : 'overview'
  )
  const [seed, setSeed] = useState<Record<string, string>>(
    validStatus && (!validTab || tabParam === 'transactions') ? { status: statusParam } : {}
  )

  function go(next: PaymentTab, filters: Record<string, string> = {}) {
    setSeed(filters)
    setTab(next)
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
      <div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1 border-b border-gray-200">
          {PAYMENT_TABS.map((t) => (
            <button
              key={t}
              onClick={() => go(t)}
              className={cn(
                'border-b-2 px-3 py-2 text-sm transition-colors duration-150',
                tab === t
                  ? 'border-[#1F6F5F] font-medium text-[#1F6F5F]'
                  : 'border-transparent text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-700'
              )}
            >
              {TAB_LABEL[t]}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
        <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-gray-500">{TAB_HINT[tab]}</p>
      </div>

      {tab === 'overview' && <PaymentsOverview onOpen={go} />}
      {tab === 'transactions' && <TransactionsTab initial={seed as Partial<TransactionFilters>} />}
      {tab === 'payouts' && <PayoutsTab initial={seed as Partial<PayoutFilters>} />}
      {tab === 'refunds' && <RefundsTab initial={seed as Partial<RefundFilters>} />}
      {tab === 'reports' && <ReportsTab />}
    </div>
  )
}

'use client'

import { useState } from 'react'
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'
import { useSWRConfig } from 'swr'
import { toast } from 'sonner'
import DataTable, { DataTableColumn } from '@/components/shared/DataTable'
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/Badge'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/Button'
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/Input'
import { LEDGER_METHODS, LEDGER_STATUSES } from '@/lib/payment-constants'
import { usePermission } from '@/hooks/usePermission'
import {
  DEFAULT_TRANSACTION_FILTERS,
  buildExportQuery,
  useFilterState,
  useTransactions,
} from '@/hooks/usePayments'
import type { TransactionFilters, TransactionRow } from '@/types/payment'
import RefundDialog from './RefundDialog'

  const [refundRow, setRefundRow] = useState<TransactionRow | null>(null)

    },

    { key: 'gross', header: 'Gross', render: (t) => formatPkr(t.gross, t.currency) },
    { key: 'fee', header: 'Platform fee', render: (t) => formatPkr(t.platformFee, t.currency) },
    { key: 'worker_amt', header: 'Worker amount', render: (t) => formatPkr(t.workerAmount, t.currency) },

      key: 'status',
      header: 'Status',
      render: (t) => (
        <div className="leading-tight">
          <Badge tone={LEDGER_STATUS_TONE[t.status]}>{LEDGER_STATUS_LABEL[t.status]}</Badge>
          {t.status === 'FAILED' && t.failureReason && (
            <span className="mt-0.5 block max-w-[180px] truncate text-[11px] text-red-600" title={t.failureReason}>
              {t.failureReason}
            </span>
          )}
          {t.refunded > 0 && (
            <span className="mt-0.5 block text-[11px] text-gray-400">
              âˆ’ {formatPkr(t.refunded, t.currency)} refunded
            </span>
          )}
        </div>
      ),
    },
    {
      key: 'gateway',
      header: 'Gateway ref',
      render: (t) => {
        const ref = t.gatewayTransactionId ?? t.gatewayOrderId
        return ref ? (
          <span className="font-mono text-[11px] text-gray-500" title={ref}>
            {ref.length > 16 ? `${ref.slice(0, 16)}â€¦` : ref}
          </span>
        ) : (
          'â€”'
        )
      },
    },
    {
      key: 'actions',
      header: '',
      render: (t) =>
        canRefund && t.paymentId && t.refundable > 0 ? (
          <Button
            size="sm"
            variant="secondary"
            onClick={(e) => {
              e.stopPropagation()
              setRefundRow(t)
            }}

            Refund

        ) : null,

          <a
            href={exportHref}
            className="flex h-9 items-center rounded-md border border-gray-300 bg-white px-3 text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-50"
          >
            Export CSV
          </a>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-white p-3">
        <div className="min-w-[240px] flex-1">
          <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-medium text-gray-700">Search</label>
          <Input
            placeholder="Booking ID, customer, worker, phone or gateway ref"
            value={filters.search}
            onChange={(e) => update({ search: e.target.value })}
          />
        </div>

        <Field label="Status">
          <select
            className={SELECT_CLASS}
            value={filters.status}
            onChange={(e) => update({ status: e.target.value as TransactionFilters['status'] })}
          >
            <option value="all">All</option>
            {LEDGER_STATUSES.map((s) => (

        </Field>
        <Field label="Max amount (PKR)">
          <Input type="number" min={0} className="w-28" value={filters.amountMax} onChange={(e) => update({ amountMax: e.target.value })} />

        <Field label="From">

        </Field>
        <Field label="To">
          <Input type="date" value={filters.dateTo ?? ''} onChange={(e) => update({ dateTo: e.target.value || null })} />
        </Field>

        <button onClick={reset} className="h-9 px-3 text-sm text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-700">
          Clear filters
        </button>
      </div>

      {transactions && transactions.total > 0 && (
        <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">
          Filtered total: gross <strong className="text-gray-800">{formatPkr(transactions.totals.gross)}</strong>
          {' Â· '}platform fees <strong className="text-gray-800">{formatPkr(transactions.totals.platformFee)}</strong>
          {' Â· '}worker amount <strong className="text-gray-800">{formatPkr(transactions.totals.workerAmount)}</strong>
        </p>
      )}

      <DataTable
        columns={columns}
        rows={transactions?.data ?? []}
        rowKey={(t) => t.paymentId ?? t.bookingId}
        isLoading={isLoading}
        error={error}
        onRetry={() => mutate()}
        emptyMessage="No transactions match these filters."
        onRowClick={(t) => router.push(`/bookings/${t.bookingId}`)}
        rowClassName={(t) => (t.status === 'FAILED' ? 'bg-red-50! even:bg-red-50! hover:bg-red-100!' : '')}
      />

      {transactions && (
        <Pager
          page={filters.page}
          pageSize={filters.pageSize}
          total={transactions.total}
          onPage={(page) => update({ page })}
        />
      )}

      {refundRow && (
        <RefundDialog
          row={refundRow}
          onClose={() => setRefundRow(null)}
          onDone={() => {
            toast.success('Refund queued. payment-service will send it to the customer.')
            globalMutate((key) => typeof key === 'string' && key.startsWith('/api/admin/payments'))
          }}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

// components/payments/RefundDialog.tsx
'use client'

import { useState } from 'react'
import ConfirmDialog from '@/components/shared/ConfirmDialog'
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/Input'
import { paymentActions } from '@/hooks/usePayments'
import type { TransactionRow } from '@/types/payment'
import { formatPkr, methodLabel, shortId } from './payment-ui'

export default function RefundDialog({
  row,
  onClose,
  onDone,
}: {
  row: TransactionRow
  onClose: () => void
  onDone: () => void
}) {
  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(String(row.refundable))
  const parsed = Math.round(Number(amount) * 100) / 100

  return (
    <ConfirmDialog
      open
      title={`Refund booking ${shortId(row.bookingId)}?`}
      description={`Paid by ${row.userName ?? 'the customer'} via ${methodLabel(row.method)}. The refund goes back to that same method and takes 3â€“5 business days.`}
      requireReason
      confirmLabel="Issue refund"
      onClose={onClose}
      onConfirm={async (reason) => {
        if (!Number.isFinite(parsed) || parsed <= 0) throw new Error('Enter an amount above zero')
        if (parsed > row.refundable) {
          throw new Error(`You can refund at most ${formatPkr(row.refundable, row.currency)}`)
        }
        await paymentActions.refund({ paymentId: row.paymentId!, amount: parsed, reason: reason! })
        onDone()
      }}
    >
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
        <Input
          label={`Refund amount (${row.currency})`}
          type="number"
          min={1}
          max={row.refundable}
          step="0.01"
          value={amount}
          onChange={(e) => setAmount(e.target.value)}
          helperText={`Up to ${formatPkr(row.refundable, row.currency)} of ${formatPkr(row.gross, row.currency)}.`}
        />
        {row.status === 'RELEASED' && (
          <p className="rounded-md bg-red-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-red-700">
            The worker has already been credited for this booking. Only use this for
            finance-approved exceptions. Customer complaints should go through Disputes.
          </p>
        )}
      </div>
    </ConfirmDialog>
  )
}

export function Person({ name, phone }: { name: string | null; phone: string | null }) {
  return (
    <div className="leading-tight">
      <span className="font-medium text-gray-900">{name ?? 'Unknown'}</span>
      {phone && <span className="block font-mono text-[11px] text-gray-400">{phone}</span>}
    </div>
  )
}

export function Pager({
  page,
  pageSize,
  total,
  onPage,
}: {
  page: number
  pageSize: number
  total: number
  onPage: (page: number) => void
}) {
  if (total === 0) return null
  const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / pageSize))
  const start = (page - 1) * pageSize + 1
  const end = Math.min(page * pageSize, total)

  return (
    <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-sm text-gray-500">
      <span>
        {start}â€“{end} of {total.toLocaleString()}
      </span>
      <div className="flex gap-2">
        <button
          className="h-9 rounded-md border border-gray-300 px-3 disabled:opacity-40"
          disabled={page <= 1}
          onClick={() => onPage(page - 1)}
        >
          Previous
        </button>
        <button
          className="h-9 rounded-md border border-gray-300 px-3 disabled:opacity-40"
          disabled={page >= totalPages}
          onClick={() => onPage(page + 1)}
        >
          Next
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

// hooks/usePayments.ts
import useSWR from 'swr'
import { useState } from 'react'
import { fetcher } from '@/lib/swr-fetcher'
import type {
  ListResponse,
  OverviewPeriod,
  PaymentOverview,
  PayoutFilters,
  PayoutRow,
  RefundFilters,
  RefundRow,
  RevenueSeries,
  TransactionFilters,
  TransactionList,
} from '@/types/payment'

export const DEFAULT_TRANSACTION_FILTERS: TransactionFilters = {
  search: '',
  status: 'all',
  method: 'all',
  city: 'all',
  amountMin: '',
  amountMax: '',
  dateFrom: null,
  dateTo: null,
  page: 1,
  pageSize: 25,
}

export const DEFAULT_PAYOUT_FILTERS: PayoutFilters = {
  search: '',
  status: 'all',
  method: 'all',
  dateFrom: null,
  dateTo: null,
  page: 1,
  pageSize: 25,
}

export const DEFAULT_REFUND_FILTERS: RefundFilters = {
  search: '',
  status: 'all',
  reason: 'all',
  initiatedBy: 'all',
  dateFrom: null,
  dateTo: null,
  page: 1,
  pageSize: 25,
}

type Primitive = string | number | null | undefined

function toQs(filters: object, omit: string[] = []): string {
  const qs = new URLSearchParams()
  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(filters as Record<string, Primitive>)) {
    if (omit.includes(k) || v === null || v === undefined || v === '' || v === 'all') continue
    qs.set(k, String(v))
  }
  return qs.toString()
}

/** Filters only (no paging): used by the CSV export links. */
export const buildExportQuery = (filters: object) => toQs(filters, ['page', 'pageSize'])

/** One filter-state hook for all three tables. Any real change resets to page 1. */
export function useFilterState<T extends { page: number }>(defaults: T, initial: Partial<T> = {}) {
  const [filters, setFilters] = useState<T>({ ...defaults, ...initial })

  function update(patch: Partial<T>) {
    const resetsPage = Object.keys(patch).some((k) => k !== 'page')
    setFilters((prev) => ({ ...prev, ...patch, page: resetsPage ? 1 : (patch.page ?? prev.page) }))
  }

  function reset() {
    setFilters(defaults)
  }

  return { filters, update, reset }
}

export function usePaymentOverview(period: OverviewPeriod) {
  const { data, error, isLoading, mutate } = useSWR<PaymentOverview>(
    `/api/admin/payments/overview?period=${period}`,
    fetcher,
    { refreshInterval: 60_000, keepPreviousData: true }
  )
  return { overview: data, isLoading, error, mutate }
}

export function useRevenueSeries(days: 30 | 90) {
  const { data, error, isLoading } = useSWR<RevenueSeries>(
    `/api/admin/payments/revenue?days=${days}`,
    fetcher,
    { refreshInterval: 60_000, keepPreviousData: true }
  )
  return { series: data, isLoading, error }
}

export function useTransactions(filters: TransactionFilters) {
  const { data, error, isLoading, mutate } = useSWR<TransactionList>(
    `/api/admin/payments/transactions?${toQs(filters)}`,
    fetcher,
    { keepPreviousData: true, refreshInterval: 60_000 }
  )
  return { transactions: data, isLoading, error, mutate }
}

export function usePayouts(filters: PayoutFilters) {
  const { data, error, isLoading, mutate } = useSWR<ListResponse<PayoutRow>>(
    `/api/admin/payments/payouts?${toQs(filters)}`,
    fetcher,
    { keepPreviousData: true, refreshInterval: 60_000 }
  )
  return { payouts: data, isLoading, error, mutate }
}

export function useRefunds(filters: RefundFilters) {
  const { data, error, isLoading, mutate } = useSWR<ListResponse<RefundRow>>(
    `/api/admin/payments/refunds?${toQs(filters)}`,
    fetcher,
    { keepPreviousData: true, refreshInterval: 60_000 }
  )
  return { refunds: data, isLoading, error, mutate }
}

// --- Mutations ---

async function send(method: 'POST', url: string, body?: object) {
  const res = await fetch(url, {
    method,
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify(body ?? {}),
  })
  const json = await res.json().catch(() => ({}))
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(json.error || 'Action failed')
  return json
}

export const paymentActions = {
  refund: (input: { paymentId: string; amount: number; reason: string }) =>
    send('POST', '/api/admin/payments/refunds', input),
  retryPayout: (payoutId: string) =>
    send('POST', `/api/admin/payments/payouts/${payoutId}/retry`),
}

// lib/payments-api.ts
// Server-only helpers for the /api/admin/payments/* routes.
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server'
import { CITY_SQL } from '@/lib/bookings-api'
import { PaymentServiceError } from '@/lib/payment-service'
import {
  LEDGER_METHODS,
  LEDGER_STATUSES,
  PAYOUT_STATUSES,
  REFUND_REASONS,
  REFUND_STATUSES,
  REFUNDABLE_STATUSES,
  type LedgerMethod,
  type LedgerStatus,
} from '@/lib/payment-constants'
import type { PayoutRow, RefundRow, TransactionRow } from '@/types/payment'

export { CITY_SQL }

const DATE_RE = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/

// ---------- Errors ----------

export class PaymentActionError extends Error {
  status: number
  constructor(status: number, message: string) {
    super(message)
    this.status = status
  }
}

export function paymentsErrorResponse(err: unknown, tag: string) {
  if (err instanceof PaymentActionError) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: err.message }, { status: err.status })
  }
  if (err instanceof PaymentServiceError) {
    const status = err.status >= 500 ? 502 : err.status
    return NextResponse.json({ error: err.message, code: err.code }, { status })
  }
  const code = (err as { code?: string } | null)?.code
  if (code === '42P01' || code === '42703') {
    console.error(tag, err)
    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        error:
          'Payment tables are missing or out of date. Run the payment-service migrations on the shared database.',
      },
      { status: 503 }
    )
  }
  console.error(tag, err)
  return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Request failed' }, { status: 500 })
}

// ---------- Small utilities ----------

export const num = (v: unknown) => Number(v ?? 0)

/** Never send raw phone numbers / IBANs to the browser. */
export function maskDestination(value: string | null | undefined): string {
  if (!value) return 'â€”'
  return value.length <= 4 ? value : `â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢ ${value.slice(-4)}`
}

export function parsePaging(sp: URLSearchParams) {
  const page = Math.max(1, parseInt(sp.get('page') || '1', 10) || 1)
  const pageSize = Math.min(100, Math.max(1, parseInt(sp.get('pageSize') || '25', 10) || 25))
  return { page, pageSize, offset: (page - 1) * pageSize }
}

export function pagedResponse<T>(data: T[], total: number, page: number, pageSize: number) {
  return { data, page, pageSize, total, totalPages: Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / pageSize)) }
}

/** from/to default to the last 365 days. */
export function dateRange(sp: URLSearchParams): { from: string; to: string } {
  const now = new Date()
  const yearAgo = new Date(now.getTime() - 365 * 86_400_000)
  const f = sp.get('dateFrom')
  const t = sp.get('dateTo')
  return {
    from: f && DATE_RE.test(f) ? f : yearAgo.toISOString().slice(0, 10),
    to: t && DATE_RE.test(t) ? t : now.toISOString().slice(0, 10),
  }
}

function whereBuilder() {
  const where: string[] = []
  const params: unknown[] = []
  const push = (v: unknown) => {
    params.push(v)
    return `$${params.length}`
  }
  return { where, params, push }
}
const toWhereSql = (where: string[]) => (where.length ? `WHERE ${where.join(' AND ')}` : '')

// ---------- CSV ----------

function csvEscape(val: unknown): string {
  const s = val === null || val === undefined ? '' : String(val)
  // Neutralise spreadsheet formula injection in text cells (names, notes).
  const safe = /^[=+\-@]/.test(s) && Number.isNaN(Number(s)) ? `'${s}` : s
  return /[",\n]/.test(safe) ? `"${safe.replace(/"/g, '""')}"` : safe
}

export function csvResponse(filename: string, header: string[], rows: unknown[][]) {
  const lines = [header.join(','), ...rows.map((r) => r.map(csvEscape).join(','))]
  // BOM so Excel opens UTF-8 names correctly
  return new NextResponse('\uFEFF' + lines.join('\n'), {
    status: 200,
    headers: {
      'Content-Type': 'text/csv; charset=utf-8',
      'Content-Disposition': `attachment; filename="${filename}-${new Date()
        .toISOString()
        .slice(0, 10)}.csv"`,
    },
  })
}

// ---------- SQL fragments ----------
// Defined once so list, totals and export can never disagree.

export const COMPLETED_SQL = `b.status::text IN ('COMPLETED','AUTO_COMPLETED')`
export const COMPLETED_AT_SQL = `COALESCE(b.completed_at, b.updated_at)`

/** Start of the current month / year in Asia/Karachi (same convention as the workers + bookings KPIs). */
export function periodStartSql(period: 'mtd' | 'ytd') {
  const unit = period === 'ytd' ? 'year' : 'month'
  return `(date_trunc('${unit}', now() AT TIME ZONE 'Asia/Karachi') AT TIME ZONE 'Asia/Karachi')`
}

export const GROSS_SQL = `COALESCE(p.booking_amount, b.estimated_total)`
export const FEE_SQL = `COALESCE(p.platform_fee, b.platform_fee)`
export const WORKER_SQL = `COALESCE(p.worker_amount, b.worker_amount)`
export const METHOD_SQL = `COALESCE(p.method::text, 'COD')`
export const LEDGER_STATUS_SQL = `COALESCE(p.status::text, 'COD_COLLECTED')`
export const TXN_DATE_SQL = `COALESCE(p.paid_at, p.created_at, b.completed_at, b.updated_at)`

export const TXN_FROM_SQL = `
  FROM bookings b
  LEFT JOIN payments p        ON p.booking_id = b.id
  LEFT JOIN users u           ON u.id = b.user_id
  LEFT JOIN workers w         ON w.id = b.worker_id
  LEFT JOIN user_addresses ua ON ua.id = b.address_id
`

export const TXN_COLUMNS_SQL = `
  b.id AS booking_id, p.id AS payment_id,
  u.id AS user_id, u.name AS user_name, u.phone AS user_phone,
  w.id AS worker_id, w.name AS worker_name, w.phone AS worker_phone,
  ${CITY_SQL} AS city,
  ${GROSS_SQL} AS gross, ${FEE_SQL} AS platform_fee, ${WORKER_SQL} AS worker_amount,
  COALESCE(p.currency, b.currency) AS currency,
  ${METHOD_SQL} AS method, ${LEDGER_STATUS_SQL} AS status,
  p.gateway_transaction_id, p.gateway_order_id, p.failure_reason,
  (SELECT COALESCE(sum(r.amount), 0) FROM refunds r
     WHERE r.payment_id = p.id AND r.status::text <> 'FAILED') AS refunded,
  ${TXN_DATE_SQL} AS txn_at
`

// ---------- Transactions ----------

export function buildTransactionWhere(sp: URLSearchParams) {
  const { where, params, push } = whereBuilder()

  // Every online payment, plus COD bookings that were actually completed.
  where.push(`(p.id IS NOT NULL OR ${COMPLETED_SQL})`)

  const search = (sp.get('search') || '').trim()
  const status = (sp.get('status') || 'all').toUpperCase()
  const method = (sp.get('method') || 'all').toUpperCase()
  const city = sp.get('city') || 'all'
  const dateFrom = sp.get('dateFrom')
  const dateTo = sp.get('dateTo')

  if (search) {
    const p = push(`%${search}%`)
    where.push(
      `(b.id::text ILIKE ${p} OR u.name ILIKE ${p} OR u.phone ILIKE ${p} OR w.name ILIKE ${p}
        OR w.phone ILIKE ${p} OR p.gateway_transaction_id ILIKE ${p} OR p.gateway_order_id ILIKE ${p})`
    )
  }
  if ((LEDGER_STATUSES as readonly string[]).includes(status)) {
    where.push(`${LEDGER_STATUS_SQL} = ${push(status)}`)
  }
  if ((LEDGER_METHODS as readonly string[]).includes(method)) {
    where.push(`${METHOD_SQL} = ${push(method)}`)
  }
  if (city !== 'all') where.push(`${CITY_SQL} = ${push(city)}`)

  const min = Number(sp.get('amountMin'))
  const max = Number(sp.get('amountMax'))
  if (sp.get('amountMin') && Number.isFinite(min)) where.push(`${GROSS_SQL} >= ${push(min)}`)
  if (sp.get('amountMax') && Number.isFinite(max)) where.push(`${GROSS_SQL} <= ${push(max)}`)

  if (dateFrom && DATE_RE.test(dateFrom)) where.push(`${TXN_DATE_SQL} >= ${push(dateFrom)}::date`)
  if (dateTo && DATE_RE.test(dateTo)) where.push(`${TXN_DATE_SQL} < (${push(dateTo)}::date + 1)`)

  return { whereSql: toWhereSql(where), params }
}

// eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any
export function mapTransactionRow(r: any): TransactionRow {
  const gross = num(r.gross)
  const refunded = num(r.refunded)
  const status = r.status as LedgerStatus
  const refundable =
    r.payment_id && REFUNDABLE_STATUSES.includes(status)
      ? Math.max(0, Math.round((gross - refunded) * 100) / 100)
      : 0

  return {
    bookingId: r.booking_id,
    paymentId: r.payment_id,
    userId: r.user_id,
    userName: r.user_name,
    userPhone: r.user_phone,
    workerId: r.worker_id,
    workerName: r.worker_name,
    workerPhone: r.worker_phone,
    city: r.city,
    gross,
    platformFee: num(r.platform_fee),
    workerAmount: num(r.worker_amount),
    currency: r.currency ?? 'PKR',
    method: r.method as LedgerMethod,
    status,
    gatewayTransactionId: r.gateway_transaction_id,
    gatewayOrderId: r.gateway_order_id,
    failureReason: r.failure_reason,
    refunded,
    refundable,
    txnAt: r.txn_at,
  }
}

// ---------- Payouts ----------

export const PAYOUT_FROM_SQL = `
  FROM worker_payouts po
  LEFT JOIN workers w  ON w.id = po.worker_id
  LEFT JOIN payments p ON p.id = po.payment_id
`

export const PAYOUT_COLUMNS_SQL = `
  po.id, po.worker_id, w.name AS worker_name, w.phone AS worker_phone,
  po.payment_id, p.booking_id, po.amount, po.currency,
  po.payout_method::text AS method, po.payout_destination,
  po.status::text AS status, po.gateway_reference, po.failure_reason,
  po.initiated_at, po.completed_at
`

export function buildPayoutWhere(sp: URLSearchParams) {
  const { where, params, push } = whereBuilder()
  const search = (sp.get('search') || '').trim()
  const status = (sp.get('status') || 'all').toUpperCase()
  const method = (sp.get('method') || 'all').toUpperCase()
  const dateFrom = sp.get('dateFrom')
  const dateTo = sp.get('dateTo')

  if (search) {
    const p = push(`%${search}%`)
    where.push(
      `(po.id::text ILIKE ${p} OR p.booking_id::text ILIKE ${p} OR w.name ILIKE ${p}
        OR w.phone ILIKE ${p} OR po.gateway_reference ILIKE ${p})`
    )
  }
  if ((PAYOUT_STATUSES as readonly string[]).includes(status)) {
    where.push(`po.status::text = ${push(status)}`)
  }
  if (/^[A-Z_]{2,30}$/.test(method)) where.push(`po.payout_method::text = ${push(method)}`)
  if (dateFrom && DATE_RE.test(dateFrom)) where.push(`po.initiated_at >= ${push(dateFrom)}::date`)
  if (dateTo && DATE_RE.test(dateTo)) where.push(`po.initiated_at < (${push(dateTo)}::date + 1)`)

  return { whereSql: toWhereSql(where), params }
}

// eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any
export function mapPayoutRow(r: any): PayoutRow {
  return {
    id: r.id,
    workerId: r.worker_id,
    workerName: r.worker_name,
    workerPhone: r.worker_phone,
    paymentId: r.payment_id,
    bookingId: r.booking_id,
    amount: num(r.amount),
    currency: r.currency ?? 'PKR',
    method: r.method,
    destination: maskDestination(r.payout_destination),
    status: r.status,
    gatewayReference: r.gateway_reference,
    failureReason: r.failure_reason,
    initiatedAt: r.initiated_at,
    completedAt: r.completed_at,
  }
}

// ---------- Refunds ----------

export const REFUND_FROM_SQL = `
  FROM refunds r
  LEFT JOIN users u        ON u.id = r.user_id
  LEFT JOIN admin_users au ON au.id = r.initiated_by
`

export const REFUND_COLUMNS_SQL = `
  r.id, r.payment_id, r.booking_id, r.user_id, u.name AS user_name, u.phone AS user_phone,
  r.amount, r.refund_percentage, r.reason::text AS reason, r.status::text AS status,
  r.refund_method::text AS method, r.refund_destination,
  r.gateway_reference, r.failure_reason,
  (CASE WHEN r.initiated_by IS NOT NULL THEN 'admin' ELSE 'system' END) AS initiated_by_kind,
  au.full_name AS admin_name, r.initiated_at, r.completed_at
`

export function buildRefundWhere(sp: URLSearchParams) {
  const { where, params, push } = whereBuilder()
  const search = (sp.get('search') || '').trim()
  const status = (sp.get('status') || 'all').toUpperCase()
  const reason = (sp.get('reason') || 'all').toUpperCase()
  const initiatedBy = sp.get('initiatedBy') || 'all'
  const dateFrom = sp.get('dateFrom')
  const dateTo = sp.get('dateTo')

  if (search) {
    const p = push(`%${search}%`)
    where.push(
      `(r.id::text ILIKE ${p} OR r.booking_id::text ILIKE ${p} OR u.name ILIKE ${p}
        OR u.phone ILIKE ${p} OR r.gateway_reference ILIKE ${p})`
    )
  }
  if ((REFUND_STATUSES as readonly string[]).includes(status)) {
    where.push(`r.status::text = ${push(status)}`)
  }
  if ((REFUND_REASONS as readonly string[]).includes(reason)) {
    where.push(`r.reason::text = ${push(reason)}`)
  }
  if (initiatedBy === 'admin') where.push('r.initiated_by IS NOT NULL')
  if (initiatedBy === 'system') where.push('r.initiated_by IS NULL')
  if (dateFrom && DATE_RE.test(dateFrom)) where.push(`r.initiated_at >= ${push(dateFrom)}::date`)
  if (dateTo && DATE_RE.test(dateTo)) where.push(`r.initiated_at < (${push(dateTo)}::date + 1)`)

  return { whereSql: toWhereSql(where), params }
}

// eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any
export function mapRefundRow(r: any): RefundRow {
  return {
    id: r.id,
    paymentId: r.payment_id,
    bookingId: r.booking_id,
    userId: r.user_id,
    userName: r.user_name,
    userPhone: r.user_phone,
    amount: num(r.amount),
    percentage: num(r.refund_percentage),
    reason: r.reason,
    status: r.status,
    method: r.method,
    destination: maskDestination(r.refund_destination),
    gatewayReference: r.gateway_reference,
    failureReason: r.failure_reason,
    initiatedBy: r.initiated_by_kind,
    adminName: r.admin_name,
    initiatedAt: r.initiated_at,
    completedAt: r.completed_at,
  }
}

// lib/payment-constants.ts

// ---------- payments.status (payment-service enum payment_status) ----------

export const PAYMENT_STATUSES = [
  'PENDING',
  'PROCESSING',
  'HELD',
  'RELEASED',
  'REFUNDED',
  'PARTIALLY_REFUNDED',
  'FAILED',
  'CANCELLED',
  'EXPIRED',
] as const

export type LedgerStatus = (typeof LEDGER_STATUSES)[number]

// ---------- payment_method_type ----------

export const PAYMENT_METHODS = [
  'JAZZCASH_WALLET',
  'JAZZCASH_CARD',
  'EASYPAISA_WALLET',
  'BANK_CARD',
  'RAAST',
  'IBFT',
] as const
export type PaymentMethod = (typeof PAYMENT_METHODS)[number]

export const LEDGER_METHODS = [...PAYMENT_METHODS, 'COD'] as const
export type LedgerMethod = (typeof LEDGER_METHODS)[number]

// ---------- payouts / refunds ----------

export const PAYOUT_STATUSES = ['PENDING', 'PROCESSING', 'COMPLETED', 'FAILED', 'REVERSED'] as const
export type PayoutStatus = (typeof PAYOUT_STATUSES)[number]

export const REFUND_STATUSES = ['PENDING', 'PROCESSING', 'COMPLETED', 'FAILED'] as const
export type RefundStatus = (typeof REFUND_STATUSES)[number]

  'ADMIN_MANUAL_REFUND',
] as const

/** Statuses an admin may refund from the ledger. payment-service has the final say. */

FILE: app\api\admin\payments\transactions\route.ts
------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// app/api/admin/payments/transactions/route.ts
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'
import { db } from '@/lib/db'
import { requirePermission } from '@/lib/rbac-guard'
import {
  FEE_SQL,
  GROSS_SQL,
  TXN_COLUMNS_SQL,
  TXN_FROM_SQL,
  WORKER_SQL,
  buildTransactionWhere,
  mapTransactionRow,
  num,
  pagedResponse,
  parsePaging,
  paymentsErrorResponse,
} from '@/lib/payments-api'
import type { TransactionList } from '@/types/payment'

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  const guard = await requirePermission('payments:view')
  if (!guard.ok) return guard.response

  const sp = req.nextUrl.searchParams
  const { whereSql, params } = buildTransactionWhere(sp)
  const { page, pageSize, offset } = parsePaging(sp)

  const listSql = `
    SELECT ${TXN_COLUMNS_SQL}
    ${TXN_FROM_SQL}
    ${whereSql}
    ORDER BY txn_at DESC, b.id
    LIMIT $${params.length + 1} OFFSET $${params.length + 2}
  `
  const aggSql = `
    SELECT count(*)::int AS total,
           COALESCE(sum(${GROSS_SQL}), 0)  AS gross,
           COALESCE(sum(${FEE_SQL}), 0)    AS fees,
           COALESCE(sum(${WORKER_SQL}), 0) AS worker
    ${TXN_FROM_SQL}
    ${whereSql}
  `

  try {
    const [listRes, aggRes] = await Promise.all([
      db.query(listSql, [...params, pageSize, offset]),
      db.query(aggSql, params),
    ])
    const a = aggRes.rows[0]
    const body: TransactionList = {
      ...pagedResponse(listRes.rows.map(mapTransactionRow), a.total, page, pageSize),
      totals: { gross: num(a.gross), platformFee: num(a.fees), workerAmount: num(a.worker) },
    }
    return NextResponse.json(body)
  } catch (err) {
    return paymentsErrorResponse(err, '[GET /api/admin/payments/transactions]')
  }
}

'use client'

import { useState } from 'react'
import { Card } from '@/components/ui/Card'
import { Sparkline } from './Sparkline'
import { ArrowUpRight, ArrowDownRight } from 'lucide-react'
import { cn } from '@/lib/cn'

interface KpiCardProps {
  label: string
  value: string
  changePct?: number | null
  /** Text printed right after the number. Default keeps the existing dashboard wording. */
  changeLabel?: string
  sparkline?: number[]
  color?: string
}

export function KpiCard({
  label,
  value,
  changePct = null,
  changeLabel = '% vs yesterday',
  sparkline = [],
  color,
}: KpiCardProps) {
  const [clicked, setClicked] = useState(false)

  const isPositive = (changePct ?? 0) >= 0
  const isColored = Boolean(color) && !clicked

  return (
    <Card
      onClick={() => setClicked((c) => !c)}
      className={cn(
        'flex min-h-[150px] flex-col gap-2 p-4 transition-colors duration-150',
        color && 'cursor-pointer'
      )}
      style={isColored ? { backgroundColor: color, borderColor: color } : undefined}
    >
      <span
        className={cn(
          'text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide',
          isColored ? 'text-white/80' : 'text-gray-500'
        )}
      >
        {label}
      </span>

      <div className="flex items-end justify-between gap-3">
        <span
          className={cn(
            'text-2xl font-semibold',
            isColored ? 'text-white' : 'text-gray-900'
          )}
        >
          {value}
        </span>

        <div className="flex h-10 w-24 items-end justify-end">
          {sparkline.length > 0 && (
            <Sparkline data={sparkline} positive={isPositive} />
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-auto h-5">
        {changePct !== null && (
          <div
            className={cn(
              'flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-medium',
              isColored
                ? 'text-white/90'
                : isPositive
                  ? 'text-[#16A34A]'
                  : 'text-[#DC2626]'
            )}
          >
            {isPositive ? (
              <ArrowUpRight size={14} />
            ) : (
              <ArrowDownRight size={14} />
            )}
            {Math.abs(changePct).toFixed(1)}{changeLabel}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </Card>
  )
}

export function KpiCardSkeleton() {
  return (
    <Card className="flex min-h-[150px] flex-col gap-2 p-4">
      <div className="h-3 w-24 animate-pulse rounded bg-gray-200" />
      <div className="h-7 w-20 animate-pulse rounded bg-gray-200" />
      <div className="mt-auto h-5 w-28 animate-pulse rounded bg-gray-200" />
    </Card>
  )
}

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: components\dashboard\KpiGrid.tsx

'use client'

import { useDashboardKpis } from '@/hooks/useDashboard'
import { KpiCard, KpiCardSkeleton } from './KpiCard'
import { Card } from '@/components/ui/Card'

function pctChange(today: number, yesterday: number): number | null {
  if (yesterday === 0) return today === 0 ? 0 : 100
  return ((today - yesterday) / yesterday) * 100
}

function formatPkr(n: number) {
  return `PKR ${n.toLocaleString('en-PK', { maximumFractionDigits: 0 })}`
}

function sparkArray(points: { day_total: number }[] | undefined) {
  return (points ?? []).map((p) => p.day_total)
}

export function KpiGrid() {
  const { data, error, isLoading } = useDashboardKpis()

  if (error) {
    return (
      <Card className="p-4 text-sm text-red-600">
        Couldn't load KPI metrics.{' '}
        <button className="underline" onClick={() => window.location.reload()}>
          Retry
        </button>
      </Card>
    )
  }

  if (isLoading || !data) {
    return (
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
        {Array.from({ length: 4 }).map((_, i) => (
          <KpiCardSkeleton key={i} />
        ))}
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
      <KpiCard
        label="Total Revenue Today"
        value={formatPkr(data.revenueToday)}
        changePct={pctChange(data.revenueToday, data.revenueYesterday)}
        sparkline={sparkArray(data.revenueSparkline)}
        color="#1F6F5F"
      />
      <KpiCard
        label="Active Bookings Now"
        value={data.activeBookingsNow.toString()}
        changePct={null}
        sparkline={sparkArray(data.activeBookingsSparkline)}
        color="#2563EB"
      />
      <KpiCard
        label="New Signups Today"
        value={data.newSignupsToday.toString()}
        changePct={pctChange(data.newSignupsToday, data.newSignupsYesterday)}
        sparkline={sparkArray(data.signupsSparkline)}
        color="#D97706"
      />
      <KpiCard
        label="Pending Verifications"
        value={data.pendingVerifications.toString()}
        changePct={null}
        sparkline={sparkArray(data.pendingVerificationsSparkline)}
        color="#DC2626"
      />
    </div>
  )
}

// components/dashboard/ActivityFeed.tsx
'use client'

import { Card } from '@/components/ui/Card'
import { useActivityFeed } from '@/hooks/useDashboard'
import type { PlatformEventType } from '@/types/dashboard'
import { formatDistanceToNow } from 'date-fns'

const EVENT_DOT: Record<PlatformEventType, string> = {
  'booking.created': 'bg-[#2563EB]',
  'worker.verified': 'bg-[#16A34A]',
  'payment.completed': 'bg-[#16A34A]',
  'booking.disputed': 'bg-[#DC2626]',
  'user.registered': 'bg-[#6B7280]',
}

export function ActivityFeed() {
  const { data: events, error, isLoading } = useActivityFeed()

  return (
    <Card className="flex max-h-[420px] flex-col gap-3 p-4">
      <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-gray-500">
        Live Activity
      </h2>

      {error && <p className="text-sm text-red-600">Couldn't load activity feed.</p>}

      {isLoading && !events && (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
          {Array.from({ length: 6 }).map((_, i) => (
            <div key={i} className="h-6 animate-pulse rounded bg-gray-100" />
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      {events && events.length === 0 && (
        <p className="text-sm text-gray-400">No recent activity.</p>
      )}

      <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 overflow-y-auto">
        {events?.map((event) => (
          <div key={event.id} className="flex items-start gap-2 text-sm">
            <span className={`mt-1.5 h-1.5 w-1.5 shrink-0 rounded-full ${EVENT_DOT[event.event_type]}`} />
            <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col">
              <span className="text-gray-800">{event.message}</span>
              <span className="text-xs text-gray-400">
                {formatDistanceToNow(new Date(event.created_at), { addSuffix: true })}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </Card>
  )
}

FILE: db\migrations\001_admin_auth.sql

CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "uuid-ossp";

CREATE TABLE admin_users (
  id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
  email           VARCHAR(255) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
  password_hash   VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
  full_name       VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
  role            VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL CHECK (role IN ('SUPER_ADMIN','OPS','FINANCE','SUPPORT')),
  is_active       BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
  mfa_secret      VARCHAR(255),              -- encrypted TOTP secret, null until set up
  mfa_enabled     BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
  last_login_at   TIMESTAMPTZ,
  last_login_ip   VARCHAR(50),
  failed_attempts INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
  locked_until    TIMESTAMPTZ,
  created_by      UUID REFERENCES admin_users(id),
  created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
  updated_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE admin_mfa_backup_codes (
  id          UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
  admin_id    UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES admin_users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  code_hash   VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,   -- bcrypt hash of the backup code
  used_at     TIMESTAMPTZ,
  created_at  TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE admin_audit_log (
  id            UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
  admin_id      UUID REFERENCES admin_users(id),
  admin_email   VARCHAR(255),
  action        VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
  resource_type VARCHAR(100),
  resource_id   UUID,
  old_value     JSONB,
  new_value     JSONB,
  notes         TEXT,
  ip_address    VARCHAR(50),
  user_agent    TEXT,
  created_at    TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE INDEX idx_admin_audit_log_admin_id ON admin_audit_log(admin_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_admin_audit_log_created_at ON admin_audit_log(created_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX idx_admin_users_email ON admin_users(email);
